===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 485-508
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
485 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
486 |     switch (n.getType()) {
487 |       case Token.STRING:
488 |       case Token.NUMBER:
489 |       case Token.NULL:
490 |       case Token.TRUE:
491 |       case Token.FALSE:
492 |         return true;
493 |       case Token.NOT:
494 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
495 |       case Token.VOID:
496 |       case Token.NEG:
497 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
498 |       case Token.NAME:
499 |         String name = n.getString();
500 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
501 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
502 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
503 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
504 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     return false;
508 |   }

===== M0011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-499
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
470 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
471 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
472 |     int type = n.getType();
473 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
474 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
475 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     curNode = n;
479 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
480 | 
481 |     switch (type) {
482 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
483 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
484 |         break;
485 | 
486 |       default:
487 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
488 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
489 |           // would no longer point to the true next
490 |           Node next = child.getNext();
491 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
492 |           child = next;
493 |         }
494 |         break;
495 |     }
496 | 
497 |     curNode = n;
498 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
499 |   }

===== M0016 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 504-539
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
504 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
505 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
506 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
507 | 
508 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
509 | 
510 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
511 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
512 | 
513 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
514 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
515 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
516 |     }
517 | 
518 |     curNode = n;
519 |     pushScope(n);
520 | 
521 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
522 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
523 |       // scope.
524 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
528 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
529 | 
530 |     // Args
531 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
532 | 
533 |     // Body
534 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
535 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
536 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
537 | 
538 |     popScope();
539 |   }

===== M0020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 753-790
Documentation:

Method code:
753 |     @Override
754 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
755 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
756 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
757 |       }
758 | 
759 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
760 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
761 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
762 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
763 |             reportGetter(el);
764 |             continue;
765 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
766 |             reportSetter(el);
767 |             continue;
768 |           }
769 |         }
770 | 
771 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
772 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
773 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
774 |           key.setType(Token.GET);
775 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
776 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
777 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
778 |           }
779 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
780 |           key.setType(Token.SET);
781 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
782 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
783 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
784 |           }
785 |         }
786 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
787 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
788 |       }
789 |       return node;
790 |     }

===== M0033 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 445-493
Documentation:

Method code:
445 |   @Override
446 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
447 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
448 |       return true;
449 |     }
450 | 
451 |     // Union types
452 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
453 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
454 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
455 |       return false;
456 |     }
457 | 
458 |     // record types
459 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
460 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     // Interfaces
464 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
465 |     // to the interface instance.
466 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
467 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
468 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
469 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
470 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
471 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
472 |           return true;
473 |         }
474 |       }
475 |     }
476 | 
477 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
478 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
479 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
480 |           return true;
481 |         }
482 |       }
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     // other prototype based objects
486 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
487 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
488 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
489 |       // to avoid guessing.
490 |       return true;
491 |     }
492 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
493 |   }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.aliasAndInlineArguments(Node,LinkedHashMap<String, Node>,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 230-300
Documentation:
/**
   * Inlines the arguments within the node tree using the given argument map,
   * replaces "unsafe" names with local aliases.
   *
   * The aliases for unsafe require new VAR declarations, so this function
   * can not be used in for direct CALL node replacement as VAR nodes can not be
   * created there.
   *
   * @return The node or its replacement.
   */
Method code:
230 |   private Node aliasAndInlineArguments(
231 |       Node fnTemplateRoot, LinkedHashMap<String, Node> argMap,
232 |       Set<String> namesToAlias) {
233 | 
234 |     if (namesToAlias == null || namesToAlias.isEmpty()) {
235 |       // There are no names to alias, just inline the arguments directly.
236 |       Node result = FunctionArgumentInjector.inject(
237 |           compiler, fnTemplateRoot, null, argMap);
238 |       Preconditions.checkState(result == fnTemplateRoot);
239 |       return result;
240 |     } else {
241 |       // Create local alias of names that can not be safely
242 |       // used directly.
243 | 
244 |       // An arg map that will be updated to contain the
245 |       // safe aliases.
246 |       Map<String, Node> newArgMap = Maps.newHashMap(argMap);
247 | 
248 |       // Declare the alias in the same order as they
249 |       // are declared.
250 |       List<Node> newVars = Lists.newLinkedList();
251 |       // NOTE: argMap is a linked map so we get the parameters in the
252 |       // order that they were declared.
253 |       for (Entry<String, Node> entry : argMap.entrySet()) {
254 |         String name = entry.getKey();
255 |         if (namesToAlias.contains(name)) {
256 |           if (name.equals(THIS_MARKER)) {
257 |             boolean referencesThis = NodeUtil.referencesThis(fnTemplateRoot);
258 |             // Update "this", this is only necessary if "this" is referenced
259 |             // and the value of "this" is not Token.THIS, or the value of "this"
260 |             // has side effects.
261 | 
262 |             Node value = entry.getValue();
263 |             if (value.getType() != Token.THIS
264 |                 && (referencesThis
265 |                     || NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(value, compiler))) {
266 |               String newName = getUniqueThisName();
267 |               Node newValue = entry.getValue().cloneTree();
268 |               Node newNode = NodeUtil.newVarNode(newName, newValue)
269 |                   .copyInformationFromForTree(newValue);
270 |               newVars.add(0, newNode);
271 |               // Remove the parameter from the list to replace.
272 |               newArgMap.put(THIS_MARKER,
273 |                   Node.newString(Token.NAME, newName)
274 |                       .copyInformationFromForTree(newValue));
275 |             }
276 |           } else {
277 |             Node newValue = entry.getValue().cloneTree();
278 |             Node newNode = NodeUtil.newVarNode(name, newValue)
279 |                 .copyInformationFromForTree(newValue);
280 |             newVars.add(0, newNode);
281 |             // Remove the parameter from the list to replace.
282 |             newArgMap.remove(name);
283 |           }
284 |         }
285 |       }
286 | 
287 |       // Inline the arguments.
288 |       Node result = FunctionArgumentInjector.inject(
289 |           compiler, fnTemplateRoot, null, newArgMap);
290 |       Preconditions.checkState(result == fnTemplateRoot);
291 | 
292 |       // Now that the names have been replaced, add the new aliases for
293 |       // the old names.
294 |       for (Node n : newVars) {
295 |         fnTemplateRoot.addChildToFront(n);
296 |       }
297 | 
298 |       return result;
299 |     }
300 |   }

69 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

71 |       requiresLineNumbers = false;
72 |     } else if (requiresLineNumbers) {

74 |         // The tree version of the node is really the best diagnostic
75 |         // info we have to offer here.

79 |       }
80 |     }
81 |   }

===== M0040 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1781-1844
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1781 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1782 |     if (type != node.getType()
1783 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1784 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1785 |       return false;
1786 |     }
1787 | 
1788 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1789 |       return false;
1790 |     }
1791 | 
1792 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1793 |       try {
1794 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1795 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1796 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1797 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1798 |             return false;
1799 |           }
1800 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1801 |           return false;
1802 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1803 |           return false;
1804 |         } else {
1805 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1806 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1807 |               return false;
1808 |             }
1809 |           }
1810 |         }
1811 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1812 |         return false;
1813 |       }
1814 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1815 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1816 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1817 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1818 |         return false;
1819 |       }
1820 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1821 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1822 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1823 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1824 |         return false;
1825 |       }
1826 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1827 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1828 |         return false;
1829 |       }
1830 |     }
1831 | 
1832 |     if (recurse) {
1833 |       Node n, n2;
1834 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1835 |            n != null;
1836 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1837 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1838 |           return false;
1839 |         }
1840 |       }
1841 |     }
1842 | 
1843 |     return true;
1844 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)

Method code:
1034 |   private void toString(
1035 |       StringBuilder sb,
1036 |       boolean printSource,
1037 |       boolean printAnnotations,
1038 |       boolean printType) {
1039 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
1040 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
1041 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
1042 |         sb.append(' ');
1043 |         sb.append(getString());
1044 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
1045 |         sb.append(' ');
1046 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
1047 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
1048 |         // toStringTree.
1049 |         if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
1050 |           sb.append("<invalid>");
1051 |         } else {
1052 |           sb.append(first.getString());
1053 |         }
1054 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
1055 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
1056 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
1057 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
1058 |           sb.append(' ');
1059 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
1060 |         }
1061 |         if (printSource) {
1062 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
1063 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
1064 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
1065 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
1066 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
1067 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
1068 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
1069 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
1070 |           sb.append(']');
1071 |         }
1072 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
1073 |         sb.append(' ');
1074 |         sb.append(getDouble());
1075 |       }
1076 |       if (printSource) {
1077 |         int lineno = getLineno();
1078 |         if (lineno != -1) {
1079 |           sb.append(' ');
1080 |           sb.append(lineno);
1081 |         }
1082 |       }
1083 | 
1084 |       if (printAnnotations) {
1085 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
1086 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
1087 |           int type = keys[i];
1088 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
1089 |           sb.append(" [");
1090 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
1091 |           sb.append(": ");
1092 |           String value;
1093 |           switch (type) {
1094 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
1095 |               value = "target block property";
1096 |               break;
1097 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
1098 |               value = "last local block";
1099 |               break;
1100 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
1101 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1102 |                 case BOTH:
1103 |                   value = "both";
1104 |                   break;
1105 |                 case RIGHT:
1106 |                   value = "right";
1107 |                   break;
1108 |                 case LEFT:
1109 |                   value = "left";
1110 |                   break;
1111 |                 default:
1112 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1113 |               }
1114 |               break;
1115 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1116 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1117 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1118 |                   value = "eval";
1119 |                   break;
1120 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1121 |                   value = "with";
1122 |                   break;
1123 |                 default:
1124 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1125 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1126 |               }
1127 |               break;
1128 |             default:
1129 |               value = x.toString();
1130 |               break;
1131 |           }
1132 |           sb.append(value);
1133 |           sb.append(']');
1134 |         }
1135 |       }
1136 | 
1137 |       if (printType) {
1138 |         if (jsType != null) {
1139 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1140 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1141 |             sb.append(" : ");
1142 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1143 |           }
1144 |         }
1145 |       }
1146 |     }
1147 |   }

/**
   * Replace the 'return' statement with its child expression.
   *   "return foo()" becomes "foo()" or "resultName = foo()"
   *   "return" is removed or becomes "resultName = void 0".
   *

402 |   private static void convertLastReturnToStatement(

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inlineReturnValue(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 253-284
Documentation:
/**
   * Inline a function that fulfills the requirements of
   * canInlineReferenceDirectly into the call site, replacing only the CALL
   * node.
   */
Method code:
253 |   private Node inlineReturnValue(Node callNode, Node fnNode) {
254 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
255 |     Node callParentNode = callNode.getParent();
256 | 
257 |     // NOTE: As the normalize pass guarantees globals aren't being
258 |     // shadowed and an expression can't introduce new names, there is
259 |     // no need to check for conflicts.
260 | 
261 |     // Create an argName -> expression map, checking for side effects.
262 |     Map<String, Node> argMap =
263 |         FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(
264 |             fnNode, callNode, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
265 | 
266 |     Node newExpression;
267 |     if (!block.hasChildren()) {
268 |       Node srcLocation = block;
269 |       newExpression = NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation);
270 |     } else {
271 |       Node returnNode = block.getFirstChild();
272 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(returnNode.getType() == Token.RETURN);
273 | 
274 |       // Clone the return node first.
275 |       Node safeReturnNode = returnNode.cloneTree();
276 |       Node inlineResult = FunctionArgumentInjector.inject(
277 |           null, safeReturnNode, null, argMap);
278 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(safeReturnNode == inlineResult);
279 |       newExpression = safeReturnNode.removeFirstChild();
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     callParentNode.replaceChild(callNode, newExpression);
283 |     return newExpression;
284 |   }

389 |   private void validateVar(Node n) {

392 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 550-572
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a function can be inlined at a particular call site.
   * There are several criteria that the function and reference must hold in
   * order for the functions to be inlined:
   * - It must be a simple call, or assignment, or var initialization.
   * <pre>
   *    f();
   *    a = foo();
   *    var a = foo();
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
550 |   private CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(
551 |       NodeTraversal t, Node callNode, Node fnNode, Set<String> namesToAlias) {
552 |     CallSiteType callSiteType = classifyCallSite(callNode);
553 |     if (callSiteType == CallSiteType.UNSUPPORTED) {
554 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     if (!allowDecomposition
558 |         && callSiteType == CallSiteType.DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION) {
559 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
560 |     }
561 | 
562 |     if (!callMeetsBlockInliningRequirements(
563 |             t, callNode, fnNode, namesToAlias)) {
564 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
565 |     }
566 | 
567 |     if (callSiteType == CallSiteType.DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION)  {
568 |       return CanInlineResult.AFTER_DECOMPOSITION;
569 |     } else {
570 |       return CanInlineResult.YES;
571 |     }
572 |   }

===== M0072 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.estimateCallCost(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 826-844
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets an estimate of the cost in characters of making the function call:
   * the sum of the identifiers and the separators.
   * @param referencesThis
   */
Method code:
826 |   private static int estimateCallCost(Node fnNode, boolean referencesThis) {
827 |     Node argsNode = NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode);
828 |     int numArgs = argsNode.getChildCount();
829 | 
830 |     int callCost = NAME_COST_ESTIMATE + PAREN_COST;
831 |     if (numArgs > 0) {
832 |       callCost += (numArgs * NAME_COST_ESTIMATE) + ((numArgs - 1) * COMMA_COST);
833 |     }
834 | 
835 |     if (referencesThis) {
836 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Update this if we start supporting inlining
837 |       // other functions that reference this.
838 |       // The only functions that reference this that are currently inlined
839 |       // are those that are called via ".call" with an explicit "this".
840 |       callCost += 5 + 5;  // ".call" + "this,"
841 |     }
842 | 
843 |     return callCost;
844 |   }

===== M0074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1077-1097
Documentation:

Method code:
1077 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1078 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1079 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1080 |       return s;
1081 |     }
1082 | 
1083 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1084 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1085 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1086 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1087 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1088 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1089 |       // crazy.
1090 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1091 |         sb.append(c);
1092 |       } else {
1093 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1094 |       }
1095 |     }
1096 |     return sb.toString();
1097 |   }

/**
     * Rewrite named unhoisted functions declarations to a known
     * consistent behavior so we don't to different logic paths for the same
     * code. From:
     *    function f() {}
     * to:
     *    var f = function () {};

392 |         rewriteFunctionDeclaration(n);

/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.
   *
   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:
   * <ol>
   * <li>In CodingConventionAnnotator, any name that matches the
   *     {@link CodingConvention#isConstant(String)} is annotated with an
   *     IS_CONSTANT_NAME property.
   * <li>The normalize pass renames any variable with the IS_CONSTANT_NAME
   *     annotation and that is initialized to a constant value with
   *     a variable name inlucding $$constant.
   * <li>Return true here if the variable includes $$constant in its name.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param node A NAME or STRING node
   * @return True if the variable is constant
   */
Method code:
2907 |   static boolean isConstantName(Node node) {
2908 |     return node.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
2909 |   }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.getReplacementReturnStatement(Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 437-463
Documentation:
/**
   * Replace the 'return' statement with its child expression.
   * If the result is needed (resultName != null):
   *   "return foo()" becomes "resultName = foo()"
   *   "return" becomes "resultName = void 0".
   * Otherwise:
   *   "return foo()" becomes "foo()"
   *   "return", null is returned.
   */
Method code:
437 |   private static Node getReplacementReturnStatement(
438 |       Node node, String resultName) {
439 |     Node resultNode = null;
440 | 
441 |     Node retVal = null;
442 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
443 |       // Clone the child as the child hasn't been removed
444 |       // from the node yet.
445 |       retVal = node.getFirstChild().cloneTree();
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     if (resultName == null) {
449 |       if (retVal != null) {
450 |         resultNode = NodeUtil.newExpr(retVal); // maybe null.
451 |       }
452 |     } else {
453 |       if (retVal == null) {
454 |         // A result is needed create a dummy value.
455 |         Node srcLocation = node;
456 |         retVal = NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation);
457 |       }
458 |       // Create a "resultName = retVal;" statement.
459 |       resultNode = createAssignStatementNode(resultName, retVal);
460 |     }
461 | 
462 |     return resultNode;
463 |   }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.findExpressionRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 641-669
Documentation:
/**
   * @return The statement containing the expression. null if subExpression
   *     is not contain by in by a Node where inlining is known to be possible.
   *     For example, a WHILE node condition expression.
   */
Method code:
641 |   static Node findExpressionRoot(Node subExpression) {
642 |     Node child = subExpression;
643 |     for (Node parent : child.getAncestors()) {
644 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
645 |       switch (parentType) {
646 |         // Supported expression roots:
647 |         // SWITCH and IF can have multiple children, but the CASE, DEFAULT,
648 |         // or BLOCK will be encountered first for any of the children other
649 |         // than the condition.
650 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
651 |         case Token.IF:
652 |         case Token.SWITCH:
653 |         case Token.RETURN:
654 |         case Token.VAR:
655 |           Preconditions.checkState(child == parent.getFirstChild());
656 |           return parent;
657 |         // Any of these indicate an unsupported expression:
658 |         case Token.SCRIPT:
659 |         case Token.BLOCK:
660 |         case Token.LABEL:
661 |         case Token.CASE:
662 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
663 |           return null;
664 |       }
665 |       child = parent;
666 |     }
667 | 
668 |     throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected AST structure.");
669 |   }

===== M0115 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.inject(AbstractCompiler,Node,Node,Map<String, Node>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 61-107
Documentation:

Method code:
61 |   static Node inject(AbstractCompiler compiler, Node node, Node parent,
62 |       Map<String, Node> replacements, boolean replaceThis) {
63 |     if (node.getType() == Token.NAME) {
64 |       Node replacementTemplate = replacements.get(node.getString());
65 |       if (replacementTemplate != null) {
66 |         // This should not be replacing declared names.
67 |         Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION
68 |             || parent.getType() != Token.VAR
69 |             || parent.getType() != Token.CATCH);
70 |         // The name may need to be replaced more than once,
71 |         // so we need to clone the node.
72 |         Node replacement = replacementTemplate.cloneTree();
73 |         parent.replaceChild(node, replacement);
74 |         return replacement;
75 |       }
76 |     } else if (replaceThis && node.getType() == Token.THIS) {
77 |       Node replacementTemplate = replacements.get(THIS_MARKER);
78 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(replacementTemplate);
79 |       if (replacementTemplate.getType() != Token.THIS) {
80 |         // The name may need to be replaced more than once,
81 |         // so we need to clone the node.
82 |         Node replacement = replacementTemplate.cloneTree();
83 |         parent.replaceChild(node, replacement);
84 | 
85 |         // Remove the value.  This isn't required but it ensures that we won't
86 |         // inject side-effects multiple times as it will trigger the null
87 |         // check above if we do.
88 |         if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(replacementTemplate, compiler)) {
89 |           replacements.remove(THIS_MARKER);
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         return replacement;
93 |       }
94 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
95 |       // Once we enter another scope the "this" value changes, don't try
96 |       // to replace it within an inner scope.
97 |       replaceThis = false;
98 |     }
99 | 
100 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
101 |       // We have to reassign c in case it was replaced, because the removed c's
102 |       // getNext() would no longer be correct.
103 |       c = inject(compiler, c, node, replacements, replaceThis);
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     return node;
107 |   }

790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;

===== M0122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 599-657

Method code:
599 |     @Override
600 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
601 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
602 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
603 |       if (name == null) {
604 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
605 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
606 |           errorReporter.error(
607 |             "unnamed function statement",
608 |             sourceName,

625 |             functionNode.getLp();
626 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
627 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
628 |       }
629 | 
630 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
631 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
632 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
633 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
634 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
635 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
636 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
637 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
638 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
639 |       if (fnName != null) {
640 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
641 |       } else {
642 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
643 |       }
644 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
645 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
646 | 
647 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
648 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
649 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
650 |       }
651 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
652 | 
653 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
654 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
655 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
656 |      return node;
657 |     }

===== M0130 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 783-926
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
783 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
784 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
785 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
786 |     // that we know to be safe
787 |     switch (n.getType()) {
788 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
789 |       case Token.AND:
790 |       case Token.BLOCK:
791 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
792 |       case Token.HOOK:
793 |       case Token.IF:
794 |       case Token.IN:
795 |       case Token.LP:
796 |       case Token.NUMBER:
797 |       case Token.OR:
798 |       case Token.THIS:
799 |       case Token.TRUE:
800 |       case Token.FALSE:
801 |       case Token.NULL:
802 |       case Token.STRING:
803 |       case Token.SWITCH:
804 |       case Token.TRY:
805 |       case Token.EMPTY:
806 |         break;
807 | 
808 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
809 |       case Token.THROW:
810 |         return true;
811 | 
812 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
813 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
814 |           return true;
815 |         }
816 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
817 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
818 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
819 |             return true;
820 |           }
821 |         }
822 |         return false;
823 | 
824 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
825 |       case Token.REGEXP:
826 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
827 |           return true;
828 |         }
829 |         break;
830 | 
831 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
832 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
833 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
834 |           return true;
835 |         }
836 |         break;
837 | 
838 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
839 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
840 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
841 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
842 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
843 | 
844 |       case Token.NEW:
845 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
846 |           return true;
847 |         }
848 | 
849 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
850 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
851 |           // side-effects
852 |           break;
853 |         }
854 |         return true;
855 | 
856 |       case Token.CALL:
857 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
858 |         // side effect property set.
859 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
860 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
861 |           // side-effects
862 |           break;
863 |         }
864 |         return true;
865 | 
866 |       default:
867 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
868 |           break;
869 |         }
870 | 
871 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
872 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
873 |           if (isName(assignTarget)) {
874 |             return true;
875 |           }
876 | 
877 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
878 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
879 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
880 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
881 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
882 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
883 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
884 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
885 |             return true;
886 |           }
887 | 
888 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
889 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
890 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
891 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
892 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
893 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
894 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
895 |               return false;
896 |             }
897 | 
898 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
899 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
900 |             // other objects.
901 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
902 |             // side-effect.
903 |             while (isGet(current)) {
904 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
905 |             }
906 | 
907 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
908 |           } else {
909 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
910 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
911 |             // preserve existing behavior.
912 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
913 |           }
914 |         }
915 | 
916 |         return true;
917 |     }
918 | 
919 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
920 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
921 |         return true;
922 |       }
923 |     }
924 | 
925 |     return false;
926 |   }

===== M0137 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.removeChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1804-1857
Documentation:
/** Safely remove children while maintaining a valid node structure. */
Method code:
1804 |   static void removeChild(Node parent, Node node) {
1805 |     if (isTryFinallyNode(parent, node)) {
1806 |       if (NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(getCatchBlock(parent))) {
1807 |         // A finally can only be removed if there is a catch.
1808 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1809 |       } else {
1810 |         // Otherwise only its children can be removed.
1811 |         node.detachChildren();
1812 |       }
1813 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.CATCH) {
1814 |       // The CATCH can can only be removed if there is a finally clause.
1815 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent().getParent();
1816 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1817 |       node.detachFromParent();
1818 |     } else if (isTryCatchNodeContainer(node)) {
1819 |       // The container node itself can't be removed, but the contained CATCH
1820 |       // can if there is a 'finally' clause
1821 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent();
1822 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1823 |       node.detachChildren();
1824 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
1825 |       // Simply empty the block.  This maintains source location and
1826 |       // "synthetic"-ness.
1827 |       node.detachChildren();
1828 |     } else if (isStatementBlock(parent)
1829 |         || isSwitchCase(node)) {
1830 |       // A statement in a block can simply be removed.
1831 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1832 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
1833 |       if (parent.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1834 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1835 |       } else {
1836 |         // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1837 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1838 |         // This would leave an empty VAR, remove the VAR itself.
1839 |         removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1840 |       }
1841 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LABEL
1842 |         && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
1843 |       // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1844 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1845 |       // A LABEL without children can not be referred to, remove it.
1846 |       removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1847 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.FOR
1848 |         && parent.getChildCount() == 4) {
1849 |       // Only Token.FOR can have an Token.EMPTY other control structure
1850 |       // need something for the condition. Others need to be replaced
1851 |       // or the structure removed.
1852 |       parent.replaceChild(node, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1853 |     } else {
1854 |       throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid attempt to remove node: " +
1855 |           node.toString() + " of "+ parent.toString());
1856 |     }
1857 |   }

===== M0151 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.canInlineReferenceDirectly(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 658-716
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a function can be inlined at a particular call site.
   * There are several criteria that the function and reference must hold in
   * order for the functions to be inlined:
   * 1) If a call's arguments have side effects,
   * the corresponding argument in the function must only be referenced once.
   * For instance, this will not be inlined:
   * <pre>
   *     function foo(a) { return a + a }
   *     x = foo(i++);
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
658 |   private CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceDirectly(
659 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode) {
660 |     if (!isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(fnNode)) {
661 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
662 |     }
663 | 
664 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
665 | 
666 |     // CALL NODE: [ NAME, ARG1, ARG2, ... ]
667 |     Node cArg = callNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
668 | 
669 |     // Functions called via 'call' and 'apply' have a this-object as
670 |     // the first parameter, but this is not part of the called function's
671 |     // parameter list.
672 |     if (callNode.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.NAME) {
673 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
674 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Support replace this with a value.
675 |         if (cArg == null || cArg.getType() != Token.THIS) {
676 |           return CanInlineResult.NO;
677 |         }
678 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
679 |       } else {
680 |         // ".apply" call should be filtered before this.
681 |         Preconditions.checkState(!NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectApply(callNode));
682 |       }
683 |     }
684 | 
685 |     // FUNCTION NODE -> LP NODE: [ ARG1, ARG2, ... ]
686 |     Node fnParam = NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode).getFirstChild();
687 |     while (cArg != null || fnParam != null) {
688 |       // For each named parameter check if a mutable argument use more than one.
689 |       if (fnParam != null) {
690 |         if (cArg != null) {
691 |           // Check for arguments that are evaluated more than once.
692 |           // Note: Unlike block inlining, there it is not possible that a
693 |           // parameter reference will be in a loop.
694 |           if (NodeUtil.mayEffectMutableState(cArg)
695 |               && NodeUtil.getNameReferenceCount(
696 |                   block, fnParam.getString()) > 1) {
697 |             return CanInlineResult.NO;
698 |           }
699 |         }
700 | 
701 |         // Move to the next name.
702 |         fnParam = fnParam.getNext();
703 |       }
704 | 
705 |       // For every call argument check for side-effects, even if there
706 |       // isn't a named parameter to match.
707 |       if (cArg != null) {
708 |         if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(cArg)) {
709 |           return CanInlineResult.NO;
710 |         }
711 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
712 |       }
713 |     }
714 | 
715 |     return CanInlineResult.YES;
716 |   }

/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
227 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
228 |     this.options = options;
229 |     if (errorManager == null) {
230 |       if (outStream == null) {
231 |         setErrorManager(
232 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
233 |       } else {
234 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
235 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
236 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
237 |         setErrorManager(printer);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
242 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
243 |       options.checkTypes = true;
244 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
245 |       options.checkTypes = false;
246 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
247 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
248 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
249 |       // parser type warnings.
250 |       options.setWarningLevel(
251 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
252 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
253 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
257 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
258 |       options.setWarningLevel(
259 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
260 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
261 |     }
262 | 
263 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
264 |       options.setWarningLevel(
265 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
266 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
267 |     }
268 | 
269 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
270 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
271 |     guards.add(
272 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
273 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
274 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
275 | 
276 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
277 | 
278 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
279 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
280 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
281 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
282 |     // error off.
283 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
284 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
285 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
286 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
287 |     }
288 | 
289 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
290 |   }

607 |       Preconditions.checkState(fs.hasExistingFunctionDefinition());

/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1594 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1595 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1596 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1597 |     }
1598 | 
1599 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1600 |     Node n, n2;
1601 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1602 |          res == null && n != null;
1603 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1604 |       if (node2 == null) {
1605 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1606 |       }
1607 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1608 |       if (res != null) {
1609 |         return res;
1610 |       }
1611 |     }
1612 |     return res;
1613 |   }

956 |   String jsString(String s) {
957 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
958 | 
959 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
960 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
961 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
962 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
963 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
964 |       }
965 |     }
966 |

968 |     char quote;

970 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
971 |       quote = '\'';
972 |       doublequote = "\"";
973 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
974 |     } else {
975 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
976 |       quote = '\"';
977 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
978 |       singlequote = "\'";
979 |     }
980 | 
981 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
982 |         outputCharsetEncoder);

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0172 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.replaceReturns(Node,String,String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 323-372
Documentation:
/**
   *  Convert returns to assignments and breaks, as needed.
   *  For example, with a lableName of 'foo':
   *    {
   *      return a;
   *    }
   *  becomes:
   *    foo: {
   *      a;
   *      break foo;
   *    }
   *  or
   *    foo: {
   *      resultName = a;
   *      break foo;
   *    }
   *
   * @param resultMustBeSet Whether the result must always be set to a value.
   * @return The node containing the transformed block, this may be different
   *     than the passed in node 'block'.
   */
Method code:
323 |   private static Node replaceReturns(
324 |       Node block, String resultName, String labelName,
325 |       boolean resultMustBeSet) {
326 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(block);
327 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(labelName);
328 | 
329 |     Node root = block;
330 | 
331 |     boolean hasReturnAtExit = false;
332 |     int returnCount = NodeUtil.getNodeTypeReferenceCount(
333 |         block, Token.RETURN, new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
334 |     if (returnCount > 0) {
335 |       hasReturnAtExit = hasReturnAtExit(block);
336 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Simpler not to special case this,
337 |       // and let it be optimized later.
338 |       if (hasReturnAtExit) {
339 |         convertLastReturnToStatement(block, resultName);
340 |         returnCount--;
341 |       }
342 | 
343 |       if (returnCount > 0) {
344 |         // A label and breaks are needed.
345 | 
346 |         // Add the breaks
347 |         replaceReturnWithBreak(block, null, resultName, labelName);
348 | 
349 |         // Add label
350 |         Node label = new Node(Token.LABEL).copyInformationFrom(block);
351 |         Node name = Node.newString(Token.LABEL_NAME, labelName)
352 |             .copyInformationFrom(block);
353 |         label.addChildToFront(name);
354 |         label.addChildToBack(block);
355 | 
356 |         Node newRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK).copyInformationFrom(block);
357 |         newRoot.addChildrenToBack(label);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |         // The label is now the root.
361 |         root = newRoot;
362 |       }
363 |     }
364 | 
365 |     // If there wasn't an return at the end of the function block, and we need
366 |     // a result, add one to the block.
367 |     if (resultMustBeSet && !hasReturnAtExit && resultName != null) {
368 |       addDummyAssignment(block, resultName);
369 |     }
370 | 
371 |     return root;
372 |   }

===== M0189 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.replaceReturnWithBreak(Node,Node,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 480-513
Documentation:
/**
   * Replace the 'return' statement with its child expression.
   *   "return foo()" becomes "{foo(); break;}" or
   *      "{resultName = foo(); break;}"
   *   "return" becomes {break;} or "{resultName = void 0;break;}".
   */
Method code:
480 |   private static Node replaceReturnWithBreak(Node current, Node parent,
481 |       String resultName, String labelName) {
482 | 
483 |     if (current.getType() == Token.FUNCTION
484 |         || current.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
485 |       // Don't recurse into functions definitions, and expressions can't
486 |       // contain RETURN nodes.
487 |       return current;
488 |     }
489 | 
490 |     if (current.getType() == Token.RETURN) {
491 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent));
492 | 
493 |       Node resultNode = getReplacementReturnStatement(current, resultName);
494 |       Node name = Node.newString(Token.LABEL_NAME, labelName);
495 |       Node breakNode = new Node(Token.BREAK, name);
496 | 
497 |       // Replace the node in parent, and reset current to the first new child.
498 |       breakNode.copyInformationFromForTree(current);
499 |       parent.replaceChild(current, breakNode);
500 |       if (resultNode != null) {
501 |         resultNode.copyInformationFromForTree(current);
502 |         parent.addChildBefore(resultNode, breakNode);
503 |       }
504 |       current = breakNode;
505 |     } else {
506 |       for (Node c = current.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
507 |         // c may be replaced.
508 |         c = replaceReturnWithBreak(c, current, resultName, labelName);
509 |       }
510 |     }
511 | 
512 |     return current;
513 |   }

===== M0195 =====

175 |     @Override
176 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
177 |       if (!NodeUtil.isCall(node) && !NodeUtil.isNew(node)) {
178 |         return;
179 |       }
180 | 
181 |       Collection<Definition> definitions =
182 |           defFinder.getDefinitionsReferencedAt(node.getFirstChild());
183 |       if (definitions == null) {
184 |         return;

190 |         if (!noSideEffectFunctionNames.contains(lValue) &&
191 |             definitionTypeContainsFunctionType(def)) {
192 |           return;
193 |         }
194 |       }
195 |

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addArrayList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 919-932
Documentation:
/**
   * This function adds a comma-separated list as is specified by an ARRAYLIT
   * node with the associated skipIndexes array.  This is a space optimization
   * since we avoid creating a whole Node object for each empty array literal
   * slot.
   * @param firstInList The first in the node list (chained through the next
   * property).
   */
Method code:
919 |   void addArrayList(Node firstInList) {
920 |     boolean lastWasEmpty = false;
921 |     for (Node n = firstInList; n != null; n = n.getNext()) {
922 |       if (n != firstInList) {
923 |         cc.listSeparator();
924 |       }
925 |       addExpr(n, 1);
926 |       lastWasEmpty = n.getType() == Token.EMPTY;
927 |     }
928 | 
929 |     if (lastWasEmpty) {
930 |       cc.listSeparator();
931 |     }
932 |   }

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0214 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.ReferencedAfterSideEffect.hasNonLocalSideEffect(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 433-455
Documentation:
/**
     * @return Whether the node may have non-local side-effects.
     */
Method code:
433 |     private boolean hasNonLocalSideEffect(Node n) {
434 |       boolean sideEffect = false;
435 |       int type = n.getType();
436 |       // Note: Only care about changes to non-local names, specifically
437 |       // ignore VAR declaration assignments.
438 |       if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n)
439 |           || type == Token.INC
440 |           || type == Token.DEC) {
441 |         Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
442 |         // Ignore changes to local names.
443 |         if (!isLocalName(lhs)) {
444 |           sideEffect = true;
445 |         }
446 |       } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
447 |         sideEffect = NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(n);
448 |       } else if (type == Token.NEW) {
449 |         sideEffect = NodeUtil.constructorCallHasSideEffects(n);
450 |       } else if (type == Token.DELPROP) {
451 |         sideEffect = true;
452 |       }
453 | 
454 |       return sideEffect;
455 |     }

===== M0218 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 155-275
Documentation:

Method code:
155 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
156 |     switch (n.getType()) {
157 |       // Childless expressions
158 |       case Token.FALSE:
159 |       case Token.NULL:
160 |       case Token.THIS:

164 |

171 |       case Token.DEC:
172 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
173 |       case Token.VOID:
174 |       case Token.BITNOT:
175 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
176 |         return;
177 | 
178 |       // General binary ops
179 |       case Token.COMMA:
180 |       case Token.OR:
181 |       case Token.AND:
182 |       case Token.BITOR:
183 |       case Token.BITXOR:
184 |       case Token.BITAND:

190 |       case Token.GT:
191 |       case Token.LE:
192 |       case Token.GE:
193 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
194 |       case Token.IN:
195 |       case Token.LSH:
196 |       case Token.RSH:
197 |       case Token.URSH:
198 |       case Token.SUB:
199 |       case Token.ADD:
200 |       case Token.MUL:
201 |       case Token.MOD:
202 |       case Token.DIV:
203 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
204 |         return;
205 | 
206 |       // Assignments
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
218 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
219 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
220 |         return;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.HOOK:
223 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
224 |         return;
225 | 
226 |       // Node types that require special handling
227 |       case Token.STRING:
228 |         validateString(n);
229 |         return;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.NUMBER:
232 |         validateNumber(n);
233 |         return;
234 | 
235 |       case Token.NAME:
236 |         validateName(n);
237 |         return;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.GETELEM:
240 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
241 |         return;
242 | 
243 |       case Token.GETPROP:
244 |         validateGetProp(n);
245 |         return;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
248 |         validateArrayLit(n);
249 |         return;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         validateObjectLit(n);
253 |         return;
254 | 
255 |       case Token.REGEXP:
256 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
257 |         return;
258 | 
259 |       case Token.CALL:
260 |         validateCall(n);
261 |         return;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.NEW:
264 |         validateNew(n);
265 |         return;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
268 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
269 |         return;
270 | 
271 |       default:
272 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
273 |             + Node.tokenToName(n.getType()), n);
274 |     }
275 |   }

===== M0222 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inlineFunction(Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 398-507
Documentation:
/**
   * Inline a function which fulfills the requirements of
   * canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock into the call site, replacing the
   * parent expression.
   */
Method code:
398 |   private Node inlineFunction(
399 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode, String fnName) {
400 |     Node parent = callNode.getParent();
401 |     Node grandParent = parent.getParent();
402 | 
403 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Consider storing the callSite classification in the
404 |     // reference object and passing it in here.
405 |     CallSiteType callSiteType = classifyCallSite(callNode);
406 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(callSiteType != CallSiteType.UNSUPPORTED);
407 | 
408 |     boolean isCallInLoop = NodeUtil.isWithinLoop(callNode);
409 | 
410 |     // Store the name for the result. This will be used to
411 |     // replace "return expr" statements with "resultName = expr"
412 |     // to replace
413 |     String resultName = null;
414 |     boolean needsDefaultReturnResult = true;
415 |     switch (callSiteType) {
416 |       case SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
417 |         resultName = parent.getFirstChild().getString();
418 |         break;
419 | 
420 |       case VAR_DECL_SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
421 |         resultName = parent.getString();
422 |         break;
423 | 
424 |       case SIMPLE_CALL:
425 |         resultName = null;  // "foo()" doesn't need a result.
426 |         needsDefaultReturnResult = false;
427 |         break;
428 | 
429 |       case EXPRESSION:
430 |         resultName = getUniqueResultName();
431 |         // The intermediary result has a default value of "undefined", so
432 |         // we only need to set the implicit return value if we are in a loop
433 |         // and the variable maybe reused.
434 |         needsDefaultReturnResult = isCallInLoop;
435 |         break;
436 | 
437 |       case DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION:
438 |         throw new IllegalStateException(
439 |             "Decomposable expressions must decomposed before inlining.");
440 | 
441 |       default:
442 |         throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected call site type.");
443 |     }
444 | 
445 |     FunctionToBlockMutator mutator = new FunctionToBlockMutator(
446 |         compiler, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
447 | 
448 |     Node newBlock = mutator.mutate(
449 |         fnName, fnNode, callNode, resultName,
450 |         needsDefaultReturnResult, isCallInLoop);
451 | 
452 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Create a common mutation function that
453 |     // can replace either a VAR name assignment, assignment expression or
454 |     // a EXPR_RESULT.
455 |     Node greatGrandParent = grandParent.getParent();
456 |     switch (callSiteType) {
457 |       case VAR_DECL_SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
458 |         // Remove the call from the name node.
459 |         parent.removeChild(parent.getFirstChild());
460 |         Preconditions.checkState(parent.getFirstChild() == null);
461 |         // Add the call, after the VAR.
462 |         greatGrandParent.addChildAfter(newBlock, grandParent);
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       case SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
466 |         // The assignment is now part of the inline function so
467 |         // replace it completely.
468 |         Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(grandParent));
469 |         greatGrandParent.replaceChild(grandParent, newBlock);
470 |         break;
471 | 
472 |       case SIMPLE_CALL:
473 |         // If nothing is looking at the result just replace the call.
474 |         Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent));
475 |         grandParent.replaceChild(parent, newBlock);
476 |         break;
477 | 
478 |       case EXPRESSION:
479 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Maybe change this so that movable and decomposable
480 |         // expressions are handled the same way: The call is moved and
481 |         // then handled by one the three basic cases, rather than
482 |         // introducing a new case.
483 |         Node injectionPoint = ExpressionDecomposer.findInjectionPoint(callNode);
484 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(injectionPoint);
485 |         Node injectionPointParent = injectionPoint.getParent();
486 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(injectionPointParent);
487 |         Preconditions.checkState(
488 |             NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(injectionPointParent));
489 | 
490 |         // Declare the intermediate result name.
491 |         newBlock.addChildrenToFront(
492 |             NodeUtil.newVarNode(resultName, null)
493 |                  .copyInformationFromForTree(callNode));
494 |         // Inline the function before the selected injection point (before
495 |         // the call).
496 |         injectionPointParent.addChildBefore(newBlock, injectionPoint);
497 |         // Replace the call site with a reference to the intermediate
498 |         // result name.
499 |         parent.replaceChild(callNode, Node.newString(Token.NAME, resultName));
500 |         break;
501 | 
502 |       default:
503 |         throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected call site type.");
504 |     }
505 | 
506 |     return newBlock;
507 |   }

/**
   * Construct a new abstract source file.
   *
   * @param fileName The file name of the source file. It does not necessarily
   *     need to correspond to a real path. But it should be unique. Will
   *     appear in warning messages emitted by the compiler.
   */
Method code:
86 |   public SourceFile(String fileName) {
87 |     if (fileName == null || fileName.isEmpty()) {

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 877-890
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
877 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
878 |     if (item == null) {
879 |       return null;
880 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
881 |       return item.getNext();
882 |     } else {
883 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
884 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
885 |         return item.chain(result);
886 |       } else {
887 |         return item;
888 |       }
889 |     }
890 |   }

===== M0269 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.doStatementNormalizations(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 433-454
Documentation:
/**
     * Do normalizations that introduce new siblings or parents.
     */
Method code:
433 |     private void doStatementNormalizations(
434 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
435 |       if (n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
436 |         normalizeLabels(n);
437 |       }
438 | 
439 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs and LABELs, as all these
440 |       // are the only legal place for VARs and FOR statements.
441 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n) || n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
442 |         extractForInitializer(n, null, null);
443 |       }
444 | 
445 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs, as all these
446 |       // are the only legal place for VARs.
447 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
448 |         splitVarDeclarations(n);
449 |       }
450 | 
451 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
452 |         moveNamedFunctions(n.getLastChild());
453 |       }
454 |     }

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.callMeetsBlockInliningRequirements(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 579-644
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a function can be inlined at a particular call site.
   * - Don't inline if the calling function contains an inner function and
   * inlining would introduce new globals.
   */
Method code:
579 |   private boolean callMeetsBlockInliningRequirements(
580 |       NodeTraversal t, Node callNode, final Node fnNode,
581 |       Set<String> namesToAlias) {
582 |     final boolean assumeMinimumCapture = this.assumeMinimumCapture;
583 | 
584 |     // Note: functions that contain function definitions are filtered out
585 |     // in isCanidateFunction.
586 | 
587 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Determining if the called function contains VARs
588 |     // or if the caller contains inner functions accounts for 20% of the
589 |     // runtime cost of this pass.
590 | 
591 |     // Don't inline functions with var declarations into a scope with inner
592 |     // functions as the new vars would leak into the inner function and
593 |     // cause memory leaks.
594 |     boolean fnContainsVars = NodeUtil.has(
595 |         NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode),
596 |         new NodeUtil.MatchDeclaration(),
597 |         new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
598 |     boolean forbidTemps = false;
599 |     if (!t.inGlobalScope()) {
600 |       Node fnCaller = t.getScopeRoot();
601 |       Node fnCallerBody = fnCaller.getLastChild();
602 | 
603 |       // Don't allow any new vars into a scope that contains eval or one
604 |       // that contains functions (excluding the function being inlined).
605 |       Predicate<Node> match = new Predicate<Node>(){
606 |         @Override
607 |         public boolean apply(Node n) {
608 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
609 |             return n.getString().equals("eval");
610 |           }
611 |           if (!assumeMinimumCapture && n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
612 |             return n != fnNode;
613 |           }
614 |           return false;
615 |         }
616 |       };
617 |       forbidTemps = NodeUtil.has(fnCallerBody,
618 |           match, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION);
619 |     }
620 | 
621 |     if (fnContainsVars && forbidTemps) {
622 |       return false;
623 |     }
624 | 
625 |     // If the caller contains functions or evals, verify we aren't adding any
626 |     // additional VAR declarations because aliasing is needed.
627 |     if (forbidTemps) {
628 |       Map<String, Node> args =
629 |           FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(
630 |               fnNode, callNode, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
631 |       boolean hasArgs = !args.isEmpty();
632 |       if (hasArgs) {
633 |         // Limit the inlining
634 |         Set<String> allNamesToAlias = Sets.newHashSet(namesToAlias);
635 |         FunctionArgumentInjector.maybeAddTempsForCallArguments(
636 |             fnNode, args, allNamesToAlias, compiler.getCodingConvention());
637 |         if (!allNamesToAlias.isEmpty()) {
638 |           return false;
639 |         }
640 |       }
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     return true;
644 |   }

===== M0281 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.ReferencedAfterSideEffect.visit(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 395-428
Documentation:

Method code:
395 |     @Override
396 |     public void visit(Node n) {
397 |       // If we are exiting a loop.
398 |       if (NodeUtil.isLoopStructure(n)) {
399 |         loopsEntered--;
400 |         if (!inLoop() && !sideEffectSeen) {
401 |           // Now that the loops has been fully traversed and
402 |           // no side-effects have been seen, throw away
403 |           // the references seen in them.
404 |           parametersReferenced.clear();
405 |         }
406 |       }
407 | 
408 |       if (!sideEffectSeen) {
409 |         // Look for side-effects.
410 |         if (hasNonLocalSideEffect(n)) {
411 |           sideEffectSeen = true;
412 |         }
413 |       }
414 | 
415 |       // If traversing the nodes of a loop save any references
416 |       // that are seen.
417 |       if (inLoop() || sideEffectSeen) {
418 |         // Record references to parameters.
419 |         if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
420 |           String name = n.getString();
421 |           if (parameters.contains(name)) {
422 |             parametersReferenced.add(name);
423 |           }
424 |         } else if (n.getType() == Token.THIS) {
425 |           parametersReferenced.add(THIS_MARKER);
426 |         }
427 |       }
428 |     }
