===== M0293 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 513-531
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks if the given function matches the criteria for an inlinable
   * function, and if so, adds it to our set of inlinable functions.
   */
Method code:
513 |   boolean isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(Node fnNode) {
514 |     // Only inline single-statement functions
515 |     Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
516 | 
517 |     // Check if this function is suitable for direct replacement of a CALL node:
518 |     // a function that consists of single return that returns an expression.
519 |     if (!block.hasChildren()) {
520 |       // special case empty functions.
521 |       return true;
522 |     } else if (block.hasOneChild()) {
523 |       // Only inline functions that return something.
524 |       if (block.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.RETURN
525 |           && block.getFirstChild().getFirstChild() != null) {
526 |         return true;
527 |       }
528 |     }
529 | 
530 |     return false;
531 |   }

===== M0299 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.CallVisitor.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 388-420
Documentation:

Method code:
388 |     @Override
389 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
390 |       switch (n.getType()) {
391 |         // Function calls
392 |         case Token.CALL:
393 |           Node child = n.getFirstChild();
394 |           String name = null;
395 |           // NOTE: The normalization pass insures that local names do not
396 |           // collide with global names.
397 |           if (child.getType() == Token.NAME) {
398 |             name = child.getString();
399 |           } else if (child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
400 |             name = anonFunctionMap.get(child);
401 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(n)) {
402 |             Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isGet(child));
403 |             Node fnIdentifingNode = child.getFirstChild();
404 |             if (fnIdentifingNode.getType() == Token.NAME) {
405 |               name = fnIdentifingNode.getString();
406 |             } else if (fnIdentifingNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
407 |               name = anonFunctionMap.get(fnIdentifingNode);
408 |             }
409 |           }
410 | 
411 |           if (name != null) {
412 |             FunctionState fs = functionMap.get(name);
413 |             // Only visit call-sites for functions that can be inlined.
414 |             if (fs != null) {
415 |               callback.visitCallSite(t, n, parent, fs);
416 |             }
417 |           }
418 |           break;
419 |       }
420 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.canNameValueChange(Node,Node)

/**
   * This is similar to NodeUtil.isLValue except that object properties and
   * array member modification aren't important ("o" in "o.a = 2" is still "o"
   * after assignment, where in as "o = x", "o" is now "x").
   *
   * This also looks for the redefinition of a name.
   *   function (x){var x;}
   *
   * @param n The NAME node in question.
   * @param parent The parent of the node.
   */
Method code:

234 |     return (type == Token.VAR || type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC ||
235 |         (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n));
236 |   }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.resolveInlineConflicts()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 735-739
Documentation:
/**
   * Size base inlining calculations are thrown off when a function that is
   * being inlined also contains calls to functions that are slated for
   * inlining.
   *
   * Specifically, a clone of the FUNCTION node tree is used when the function
   * is inlined. Calls in this new tree are not included in the list of function
   * references so they won't be inlined (which is what we want). Here we mark
   * those functions as non-removable (as they will have new references in the
   * cloned node trees).
   *
   * This prevents a function that would only be inlined because it is
   * referenced once from being inlined into multiple call sites because
   * the calling function has been inlined in multiple locations or the
   * function being removed while there are still references.
   */
Method code:
735 |   private void resolveInlineConflicts() {
736 |     for (FunctionState fs : fns.values()) {
737 |       resolveInlineConflictsForFunction(fs);
738 |     }
739 |   }

===== M0335 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 316-344
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |     @Override
317 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
318 |       switch (n.getType()) {
319 |         case Token.WHILE:
320 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
321 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
322 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
323 |             Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
324 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
325 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
326 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
327 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
328 |           }
329 |           break;
330 | 
331 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
332 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
333 |           break;
334 | 
335 |         case Token.NAME:
336 |         case Token.STRING:
337 |         case Token.GET:
338 |         case Token.SET:
339 |           if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalizedObfuscated()) {
340 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
341 |           }
342 |           break;
343 |       }
344 |     }

===== M0342 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.addChildBefore(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 746-761
Documentation:
/**
   * Add 'child' before 'node'.
   */
Method code:
746 |   public void addChildBefore(Node newChild, Node node) {
747 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(node != null,
748 |         "The existing child node of the parent should not be null.");
749 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
750 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
751 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
752 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
753 |     if (first == node) {
754 |       newChild.parent = this;
755 |       newChild.next = first;
756 |       first = newChild;
757 |       return;
758 |     }
759 |     Node prev = getChildBefore(node);

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.getNonEmptyChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1103-1114
Documentation:
/**
   * @param maxCount The maximum number of children to look for.
   * @return The number of children of this node that are non empty up to
   * maxCount.
   */
Method code:
1103 |   private static int getNonEmptyChildCount(Node n, int maxCount) {
1104 |     int i = 0;
1105 |     Node c = n.getFirstChild();
1106 |     for (; c != null && i < maxCount; c = c.getNext()) {
1107 |       if (c.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
1108 |         i += getNonEmptyChildCount(c, maxCount-i);
1109 |       } else if (c.getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
1110 |         i++;
1111 |       }
1112 |     }
1113 |     return i;
1114 |   }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2513-2523
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2513 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2514 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2515 |     int len = s.length();
2516 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2517 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2518 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2519 |         return false;
2520 |       }
2521 |     }
2522 |     return true;
2523 |   }

===== M0383 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.opToStr(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2211-2256
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator
   */
Method code:
2211 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
2212 |     switch (operator) {
2213 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
2214 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
2215 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
2216 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
2217 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";
2218 |       case Token.SHEQ: return "===";
2219 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
2220 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
2221 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
2222 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
2223 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
2224 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
2225 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
2226 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
2227 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
2228 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";

/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
465 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
466 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
467 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
468 |           lineIndex++;
469 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
470 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
471 |         } else {
472 |           startNewLine();
473 |         }
474 |       }
475 |     }

===== M0419 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

Method code:
2912 |   static boolean isConstantByConvention(
2913 |       CodingConvention convention, Node node, Node parent) {
2914 |     String name = node.getString();
2915 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
2916 |         node == parent.getLastChild()) {
2917 |       return convention.isConstantKey(name);

===== M0421 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.canBeSideEffected(Node,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1110-1142
Documentation:
/**
   * @param knownConstants A set of names known to be constant value at
   * node 'n' (such as locals that are last written before n can execute).
   * @return Whether the tree can be affected by side-effects or
   * has side-effects.
   */
Method code:
1110 |   static boolean canBeSideEffected(Node n, Set<String> knownConstants) {
1111 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1112 |       case Token.CALL:
1113 |       case Token.NEW:
1114 |         // Function calls or constructor can reference changed values.
1115 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Add some mechanism for determining that functions
1116 |         // are unaffected by side effects.
1117 |         return true;
1118 |       case Token.NAME:
1119 |         // Non-constant names values may have been changed.
1120 |         return !isConstantName(n)
1121 |             && !knownConstants.contains(n.getString());
1122 | 
1123 |       // Properties on constant NAMEs can still be side-effected.
1124 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1125 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1126 |         return true;
1127 | 
1128 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1129 |         // Function expression are not changed by side-effects,
1130 |         // and function declarations are not part of expressions.
1131 |         Preconditions.checkState(isFunctionExpression(n));
1132 |         return false;
1133 |     }
1134 | 
1135 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
1136 |       if (canBeSideEffected(c, knownConstants)) {
1137 |         return true;
1138 |       }
1139 |     }
1140 | 
1141 |     return false;
1142 |   }

193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:

251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);

===== M0441 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.findModifiedParameters(Node,Node,Set<String>,Set<String>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 195-219
Documentation:
/**
   * Check for uses of the named value that imply a pass-by-value
   * parameter is expected.  This is used to prevent cases like:
   *
   *   function (x) {
   *     x=2;
   *     return x;
   *   }
   *
   * We don't want "undefined" to be substituted for "x", and get
   *   undefined=2
   *
   * @param n The node in question.
   * @param parent The parent of the node.
   * @param names The set of names to check.
   * @param unsafe The set of names that require aliases.
   * @param inInnerFunction Whether the inspection is occurring on a inner
   *     function.
   */
Method code:
195 |   private static Set<String> findModifiedParameters(
196 |       Node n, Node parent, Set<String> names, Set<String> unsafe,
197 |       boolean inInnerFunction) {
198 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(unsafe != null);
199 |     if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
200 |       if (names.contains(n.getString())) {
201 |         if (inInnerFunction || canNameValueChange(n, parent)) {
202 |           unsafe.add(n.getString());
203 |         }
204 |       }
205 |     } else if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
206 |       // A function parameter can not be replaced with a direct inlined value
207 |       // if it is referred to by an inner function. The inner function
208 |       // can out live the call we are replacing, so inner function must
209 |       // capture a unique name.  This approach does not work within loop
210 |       // bodies so those are forbidden elsewhere.
211 |       inInnerFunction = true;
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     for (Node c : n.children()) {
215 |       findModifiedParameters(c, n, names, unsafe, inInnerFunction);
216 |     }
217 | 
218 |     return unsafe;
219 |   }

===== M0451 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
469 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
470 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
471 |          current != null;
472 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
473 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
474 |         return true;
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

===== M0466 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0468 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0471 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

/**
   * @param fnName The name to use when preparing human readable names.
   * @param fnNode The function to prepare.
   * @param callNode The call node that will be replaced.
   * @param resultName Function results should be assigned to this name.
   * @param needsDefaultResult Whether the result value must be set.
   * @param isCallInLoop Whether the function body must be prepared to be
   *   injected into the body of a loop.

   *   as a statement into another code block.

Method code:
63 |   Node mutate(String fnName, Node fnNode, Node callNode,
64 |       String resultName, boolean needsDefaultResult, boolean isCallInLoop) {
65 |     Node newFnNode = fnNode.cloneTree();
66 |     // Now that parameter names have been replaced, make sure all the local
67 |     // names are unique, to allow functions to be inlined multiple times
68 |     // without causing conflicts.

79 |         FunctionArgumentInjector.findModifiedParameters(newFnNode);

81 |         FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(
82 |             newFnNode, callNode, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
83 |     boolean hasArgs = !args.isEmpty();
84 |     if (hasArgs) {
85 |       FunctionArgumentInjector.maybeAddTempsForCallArguments(
86 |           newFnNode, args, namesToAlias, compiler.getCodingConvention());
87 |     }
88 | 
89 |     Node newBlock = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(newFnNode);
90 |     // Make the newBlock insertable .
91 |     newBlock.detachFromParent();

94 |       Node inlineResult = aliasAndInlineArguments(newBlock,
95 |           args, namesToAlias);
96 |       Preconditions.checkState(newBlock == inlineResult);
97 |     }
98 |

101 |     // undefined as they would have been if the function were called, so ensure
102 |     // that they are properly initialized.
103 |     //

105 |       fixUnitializedVarDeclarations(newBlock);
106 |     }

===== M0485 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 158-262
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |     @Override
159 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
160 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
161 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
162 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
163 |       if (inExterns && NodeUtil.isName(node) && parent.getType() == Token.LP) {
164 |         return;
165 |       }
166 | 
167 |       Definition def =
168 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
169 |       if (def != null) {
170 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
171 |         if (name != null) {
172 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
173 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
174 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
175 |               !NodeUtil.isFunction(rValue)) {
176 | 
177 |             // Unhandled complex expression
178 |             Definition unknownDef =
179 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
180 |             def = unknownDef;
181 |           }
182 | 
183 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
184 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
185 |           if (inExterns) {
186 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
187 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
188 | 
189 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
190 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
191 | 
192 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
193 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
194 |             // literal key.
195 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
196 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
197 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
198 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
199 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
200 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
201 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
202 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
203 |                   }
204 |                 }
205 |               }
206 | 
207 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
208 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
209 |               }
210 |             }
211 |           }
212 | 
213 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
214 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
215 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
216 |                                                    def,
217 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
218 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
219 |                                                    inExterns));
220 |         }
221 |       }
222 | 
223 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent)) {
224 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
225 |         if (name != null) {
226 | 
227 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
228 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
229 | 
230 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
231 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
232 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
233 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
234 | 
235 |           boolean dropStub = false;
236 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
237 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
238 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
239 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
240 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
241 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
242 |                   dropStub = true;
243 |                   break;
244 |                 }
245 |               }
246 |             }
247 |           }
248 | 
249 |           if (!dropStub) {
250 |             // Incomplete definition
251 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
252 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
253 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
254 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
255 |                                                      definition,
256 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
257 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
258 |                                                      inExterns));
259 |           }
260 |         }
261 |       }
262 |     }

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(String,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 120-155
Documentation:
/**
   * @param fnName The name of this function. This either the name of the
   *  variable to which the function is assigned or the name from the FUNCTION
   *  node.
   * @param fnNode The FUNCTION node of the function to inspect.
   * @return Whether the function node meets the minimum requirements for
   * inlining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   boolean doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(
121 |       final String fnName, Node fnNode) {
122 |     Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
123 | 
124 |     // Basic restrictions on functions that can be inlined:
125 |     // 1) It contains a reference to itself.
126 |     // 2) It uses its parameters indirectly using "arguments" (it isn't
127 |     //    handled yet.
128 |     // 3) It references "eval". Inline a function containing eval can have
129 |     //    large performance implications.
130 | 
131 |     final String fnRecursionName = fnNode.getFirstChild().getString();
132 |     Preconditions.checkState(fnRecursionName != null);
133 | 
134 |     // If the function references "arguments" directly in the function
135 |     boolean referencesArguments = NodeUtil.isNameReferenced(
136 |         block, "arguments", NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION);
137 | 
138 |     // or it references "eval" or one of its names anywhere.
139 |     Predicate<Node> p = new Predicate<Node>(){
140 |       @Override
141 |       public boolean apply(Node n) {
142 |         if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
143 |           return n.getString().equals("eval")
144 |             || (!fnName.isEmpty()
145 |                 && n.getString().equals(fnName))
146 |             || (!fnRecursionName.isEmpty()
147 |                 && n.getString().equals(fnRecursionName));
148 |         }
149 |         return false;
150 |       }
151 |     };
152 | 
153 |     return !referencesArguments
154 |         && !NodeUtil.has(block, p, Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue());
155 |   }

===== M0498 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.canExposeExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 696-702
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine whether a expression is movable, or can be be made movable be
   * decomposing the containing expression.
   *
   * An subExpression is MOVABLE if it can be replaced with a temporary holding
   * its results and moved to immediately before the root of the expression.
   * There are three conditions that must be met for this to occur:
   * 1) There must be a location to inject a statement for the expression.  For
   * example, this condition can not be met if the expression is a loop
   * condition or CASE condition.
   * 2) If the expression can be affect by side-effects, there can not be a
   * side-effect between original location and the expression root.
   * 3) If the expression has side-effects, there can not be any other
   * expression that can be effected between the original location and the
   * expression root.
   *
   * An expression is DECOMPOSABLE if it can be rewritten so that an
   * subExpression is MOVABLE.
   *
   * An expression is decomposed by moving any other sub-expressions that
   * preventing an subExpression from being MOVABLE.
   *
   * @return Whether This is a call that can be moved to an new point in the
   * AST to allow it to be inlined.
   */
Method code:
696 |   DecompositionType canExposeExpression(Node subExpression) {
697 |     Node expressionRoot = findExpressionRoot(subExpression);
698 |     if (expressionRoot != null) {
699 |       return isSubexpressionMovable(expressionRoot, subExpression);
700 |     }
701 |     return DecompositionType.UNDECOMPOSABLE;
702 |   }

76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1153-1291
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1153 |   Node parseInputs() {
1154 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1155 | 
1156 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1157 |     // individual file parse trees.
1158 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1159 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1160 |     }
1161 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1162 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1163 |     }
1164 | 
1165 |     // Parse main js sources.
1166 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1167 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1168 | 
1169 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1170 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1171 | 
1172 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1173 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1174 | 
1175 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1176 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1177 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1178 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1179 |     }
1180 | 
1181 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1182 | 
1183 |     try {
1184 |       // Parse externs sources.
1185 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1186 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1187 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1188 |           return null;
1189 |         }
1190 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1191 |       }
1192 | 
1193 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1194 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1195 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1196 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1197 | 
1198 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1199 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1200 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1201 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1202 |           }
1203 |         }
1204 | 
1205 |         try {
1206 |           inputs =
1207 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1208 |               .manageDependencies(
1209 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1210 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1211 |           report(JSError.make(
1212 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1213 |           return null;
1214 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1215 |           report(JSError.make(
1216 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1217 |           return null;
1218 |         }
1219 |       }
1220 | 
1221 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1222 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1223 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1224 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1225 | 
1226 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1227 |         if (n == null) {
1228 |           continue;
1229 |         }
1230 | 
1231 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1232 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1233 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1234 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1235 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1236 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1237 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1238 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1239 | 
1240 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1241 | 
1242 |             externs.add(input);
1243 |             staleInputs = true;
1244 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1245 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1246 |             staleInputs = true;
1247 |           }
1248 |         }
1249 |       }
1250 | 
1251 |       if (staleInputs) {
1252 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1253 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1254 |       }
1255 | 
1256 |       // Build the AST.
1257 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1258 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1259 |         if (n == null) {
1260 |           continue;
1261 |         }
1262 | 
1263 |         if (devMode) {
1264 |           runSanityCheck();
1265 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1266 |             return null;
1267 |           }
1268 |         }
1269 | 
1270 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1271 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1272 | 
1273 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1274 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1275 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1276 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1277 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1278 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1279 |         }
1280 | 
1281 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1282 |       }
1283 | 
1284 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1285 |         return null;
1286 |       }
1287 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1288 |     } finally {
1289 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1290 |     }
1291 |   }

===== M0506 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.exposeExpression(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 145-235
Documentation:
/**
   * Rewrite the expression such that the sub-expression is in a movable
   * expression statement while maintaining evaluation order.
   *
   * Two types of subexpressions are extracted from the source expression:
   * 1) subexpressions with side-effects.
   * 2) conditional expressions, that contain the call, which are transformed
   * into IF statements.
   *
   * The following terms are used:
   *    expressionRoot: The top level node before which the any extracted
   *                    expressions should be placed before.
   *    nonconditionalExpr: The node that will be extracted either expres.
   *
   */
Method code:
145 |   private void exposeExpression(Node expressionRoot, Node subExpression) {
146 |     Node nonconditionalExpr = findNonconditionalParent(
147 |         subExpression, expressionRoot);
148 |     // Before extraction, record whether there are side-effect
149 |     boolean hasFollowingSideEffects = NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(
150 |         nonconditionalExpr);
151 | 
152 |     Node exprInjectionPoint = findInjectionPoint(nonconditionalExpr);
153 |     DecompositionState state = new DecompositionState();
154 |     state.sideEffects = hasFollowingSideEffects;
155 |     state.extractBeforeStatement = exprInjectionPoint;
156 | 
157 |     // Extract expressions in the reverse order of their evaluation.
158 |     for (Node grandchild = null,
159 |             child = nonconditionalExpr,
160 |             parent = child.getParent();
161 |          parent != expressionRoot;
162 |          grandchild = child,
163 |              child = parent,
164 |              parent = child.getParent()) {
165 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
166 |       Preconditions.checkState(
167 |           !isConditionalOp(parent) || child == parent.getFirstChild());
168 |       if (parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
169 |           if (isSafeAssign(parent, state.sideEffects)) {
170 |             // It is always safe to inline "foo()" for expressions such as
171 |             // "a = b = c = foo();"
172 |             // As the assignment is unaffected by side effect of "foo()"
173 |             // and the names assigned-to can not influence the state before
174 |             // the call to foo.
175 |             //
176 |             // This is not true of more complex LHS values, such as
177 |             // a.x = foo();
178 |             // next().x = foo();
179 |             // in these cases the checks below are necessary.
180 |           } else {
181 |             // Alias "next()" in "next().foo"
182 |             Node left = parent.getFirstChild();
183 |             int type = left.getType();
184 |             if (left != child) {
185 |               Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isGet(left));
186 |               if (type == Token.GETELEM) {
187 |                 decomposeSubExpressions(left.getLastChild(), null, state);
188 |               }
189 |               decomposeSubExpressions(left.getFirstChild(), null, state);
190 |             }
191 |           }
192 |       } else if (parentType == Token.CALL
193 |           && NodeUtil.isGet(parent.getFirstChild())) {
194 |         Node functionExpression = parent.getFirstChild();
195 |         decomposeSubExpressions(functionExpression.getNext(), child, state);
196 |         // Now handle the call expression
197 |         if (isExpressionTreeUnsafe(functionExpression, state.sideEffects)
198 |             && functionExpression.getFirstChild() != grandchild) {
199 |           // TODO(johnlenz): In Internet Explorer, non-javascript objects such
200 |           // as DOM objects can not be decomposed.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(allowObjectCallDecomposing(),
202 |               "Object method calls can not be decomposed.");
203 |           // Either there were preexisting side-effects, or this node has
204 |           // side-effects.
205 |           state.sideEffects = true;
206 | 
207 |           // Rewrite the call so "this" is preserved.
208 |           Node replacement = rewriteCallExpression(parent, state);
209 |           // Continue from here.
210 |           parent = replacement;
211 |         }
212 |       } else if (parentType == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
213 |         decomposeObjectLiteralKeys(parent.getFirstChild(), child, state);
214 |       } else {
215 |         decomposeSubExpressions(parent.getFirstChild(), child, state);
216 |       }
217 |     }
218 | 
219 |     // Now extract the expression that the decomposition is being performed to
220 |     // to allow to be moved.  All expressions that need to be evaluated before
221 |     // this have been extracted, so add the expression statement after the
222 |     // other extracted expressions and the original statement (or replace
223 |     // the original statement.
224 |     if (nonconditionalExpr == subExpression) {
225 |       // Don't extract the call, as that introduces an extra constant VAR
226 |       // that will simply need to be inlined back.  It will be handled as
227 |       // an EXPRESSION call site type.
228 |       // Node extractedCall = extractExpression(decomposition, expressionRoot);
229 |     } else {
230 |       Node parent = nonconditionalExpr.getParent();
231 |       boolean needResult = parent.getType() != Token.EXPR_RESULT;
232 |       Node extractedConditional = extractConditional(
233 |           nonconditionalExpr, exprInjectionPoint, needResult);
234 |     }
235 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 169-209
Documentation:
/**
   * @param t  The traversal use to reach the call site.
   * @param callNode The CALL node.
   * @param fnNode The function to evaluate for inlining.
   * @param needAliases A set of function parameter names that can not be
   *     used without aliasing. Returned by getUnsafeParameterNames().
   * @param mode Inlining mode to be used.
   * @param referencesThis Whether fnNode contains references to its this
   *     object.
   * @param containsFunctions Whether fnNode contains inner functions.
   * @return Whether the inlining can occur.
   */
Method code:
169 |   CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceToFunction(NodeTraversal t,
170 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode, Set<String> needAliases,
171 |       InliningMode mode, boolean referencesThis, boolean containsFunctions) {
172 |     // TODO(johnlenz): This function takes too many parameter, without
173 |     // context.  Modify the API to take a structure describing the function.
174 | 
175 |     // Allow direct function calls or "fn.call" style calls.
176 |     if (!isSupportedCallType(callNode)) {
177 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
178 |     }
179 | 
180 |     // Limit where functions that contain functions can be inline.  Introducing
181 |     // an inner function into another function can capture a variable and cause
182 |     // a memory leak.  This isn't a problem in the global scope as those values
183 |     // last until explicitly cleared.
184 |     if (containsFunctions) {
185 |       if (!assumeMinimumCapture && !t.inGlobalScope()) {
186 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Allow inlining into any scope without local names or
187 |         // inner functions.
188 |         return CanInlineResult.NO;
189 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isWithinLoop(callNode)) {
190 |         // An inner closure maybe relying on a local value holding a value for a
191 |         // single iteration through a loop.
192 |         return CanInlineResult.NO;
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 | 
196 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Add support for 'apply'
197 |     if (referencesThis && !NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
198 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Allow 'this' references to be replaced with a
199 |       // global 'this' object.
200 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     if (mode == InliningMode.DIRECT) {
204 |       return canInlineReferenceDirectly(callNode, fnNode);
205 |     } else {
206 |       return canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(
207 |           t, callNode, fnNode, needAliases);
208 |     }
209 |   }

/**
   * Only ".call" calls and direct calls to functions are supported.

216 |   private boolean isSupportedCallType(Node callNode) {

===== M0527 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.DefaultRedeclarationHandler.onRedeclaration(Scope,String,Node,CompilerInput)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 192-225
Documentation:

Method code:
192 |     @Override
193 |     public void onRedeclaration(
194 |         Scope s, String name, Node n, CompilerInput input) {
195 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
196 | 
197 |       // Don't allow multiple variables to be declared at the top level scope
198 |       if (scope.isGlobal()) {
199 |         Scope.Var origVar = scope.getVar(name);
200 |         Node origParent = origVar.getParentNode();
201 |         if (origParent.getType() == Token.CATCH &&
202 |             parent.getType() == Token.CATCH) {
203 |           // Okay, both are 'catch(x)' variables.
204 |           return;
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         boolean allowDupe = hasDuplicateDeclarationSuppression(n, origVar);
208 | 
209 |         if (!allowDupe) {
210 |           compiler.report(
211 |               JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
212 |                            VAR_MULTIPLY_DECLARED_ERROR,
213 |                            name,
214 |                            (origVar.input != null
215 |                             ? origVar.input.getName()
216 |                             : "??")));
217 |         }
218 |       } else if (name.equals(ARGUMENTS) && !NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         // Disallow shadowing "arguments" as we can't handle with our current
220 |         // scope modeling.
221 |         compiler.report(
222 |             JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
223 |                 VAR_ARGUMENTS_SHADOWED_ERROR));
224 |       }
225 |     }

===== M0528 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 137-149
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
137 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
138 |     if (NodeUtil.isName(node)) {
139 |       String name = node.getString();
140 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
141 |         return name;
142 |       } else {
143 |         return null;
144 |       }
145 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isGetProp(node)) {
146 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
147 |     }
148 |     return null;
149 |   }

===== M0538 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.maybeAddFunction(Function,JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 242-308
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the FunctionState object for the given function. Checks if the
   * given function matches the criteria for an inlinable function.
   */
Method code:
242 |   private void maybeAddFunction(Function fn, JSModule module) {
243 |     String name = fn.getName();
244 |     FunctionState fs = getOrCreateFunctionState(name);
245 | 
246 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Maybe "smarten" FunctionState by adding this logic
247 |     // to it?
248 | 
249 |     // If the function has multiple definitions, don't inline it.
250 |     if (fs.hasExistingFunctionDefinition()) {
251 |       fs.setInline(false);
252 |     } else {
253 |       // verify the function hasn't already been marked as "don't inline"
254 |       if (fs.canInline()) {
255 |         // store it for use when inlining.
256 |         fs.setFn(fn);
257 |         if (injector.isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(
258 |             fn.getFunctionNode())) {
259 |           fs.inlineDirectly(true);
260 |         }
261 | 
262 |         // verify the function meets all the requirements.
263 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Minimum requirement checks are about 5% of the
264 |         // runtime cost of this pass.
265 |         if (!isCandidateFunction(fn)) {
266 |           // It doesn't meet the requirements.
267 |           fs.setInline(false);
268 |         }
269 | 
270 |         // Set the module and gather names that need temporaries.
271 |         if (fs.canInline()) {
272 |           fs.setModule(module);
273 | 
274 |           Node fnNode = fn.getFunctionNode();
275 |           Set<String> namesToAlias =
276 |               FunctionArgumentInjector.findModifiedParameters(fnNode);
277 |           if (!namesToAlias.isEmpty()) {
278 |             fs.inlineDirectly(false);
279 |             fs.setNamesToAlias(namesToAlias);
280 |           }
281 | 
282 |           Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
283 |           if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(block)) {
284 |             fs.setReferencesThis(true);
285 |           }
286 | 
287 |           if (NodeUtil.containsFunction(block)) {
288 |             fs.setHasInnerFunctions(true);
289 |             // If there are inner functions, we can inline into global scope
290 |             // if there are no local vars or named functions.
291 |             // TODO(johnlenz): this can be improved by looking at the possible
292 |             // values for locals.  If there are simple values, or constants
293 |             // we could still inline.
294 |             if (!assumeMinimumCapture && hasLocalNames(fnNode)) {
295 |               fs.setInline(false);
296 |             }
297 |           }
298 |         }
299 | 
300 |         // Check if block inlining is allowed.
301 |         if (fs.canInline() && !fs.canInlineDirectly()) {
302 |           if (!blockFunctionInliningEnabled) {
303 |             fs.setInline(false);
304 |           }
305 |         }
306 |       }
307 |     }
308 |   }
