===== M0548 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-735
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
104 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
105 | 
106 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
107 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
108 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
109 | 
110 |       // Handle associativity.
111 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
112 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
113 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
114 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
119 |       } else {
120 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
121 |       }
122 |       return;
123 |     }
124 | 
125 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
126 | 
127 |     switch (type) {
128 |       case Token.TRY: {
129 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
130 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
132 | 
133 |         add("try");
134 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
135 | 
136 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
137 |         // isn't a catch block
138 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
139 |         if (catchblock != null) {
140 |           add(catchblock);
141 |         }
142 | 
143 |         if (childCount == 3) {
144 |           add("finally");
145 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         }
147 |         break;
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       case Token.CATCH:
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
152 |         add("catch(");
153 |         add(first);
154 |         add(")");
155 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.THROW:
159 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
160 |         add("throw");
161 |         add(first);
162 | 
163 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
164 |         // parse this.
165 |         cc.endStatement(true);
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.RETURN:
169 |         add("return");
170 |         if (childCount == 1) {
171 |           add(first);
172 |         } else {
173 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
174 |         }
175 |         cc.endStatement();
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.VAR:
179 |         if (first != null) {
180 |           add("var ");
181 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
182 |         }
183 |         break;
184 | 
185 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
186 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
187 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
188 |         break;
189 | 
190 |       case Token.NAME:
191 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
192 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
193 |         } else {
194 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
195 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
196 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
197 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
198 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
199 |           } else {
200 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
201 |             // precedence.
202 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
203 |           }
204 |         }
205 |         break;
206 | 
207 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
208 |         add("[");
209 |         addArrayList(first);
210 |         add("]");
211 |         break;
212 | 
213 |       case Token.LP:
214 |         add("(");
215 |         addList(first);
216 |         add(")");
217 |         break;
218 | 
219 |       case Token.COMMA:
220 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
221 |         addList(first, false, context);
222 |         break;
223 | 
224 |       case Token.NUMBER:
225 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
226 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
227 |         break;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
230 |       case Token.VOID:
231 |       case Token.NOT:
232 |       case Token.BITNOT:
233 |       case Token.POS: {
234 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
237 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
238 |         break;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       case Token.NEG: {
242 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
243 | 
244 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
245 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
246 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
247 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
248 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
249 |         } else {
250 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
251 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         break;
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       case Token.HOOK: {
258 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
259 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
260 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
261 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
262 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
263 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
264 |         addExpr(last, 1);
265 |         break;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       case Token.REGEXP:
269 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
270 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
271 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
275 | 
276 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
277 |         if (childCount == 2) {
278 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
279 |         } else {
280 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
281 |           add(regexp);
282 |         }
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.GET_REF:
286 |         add(first);
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
290 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
291 |         add(first);
292 |         add(".");
293 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
294 |         break;
295 | 
296 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
297 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
298 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
299 |         }
300 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
301 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
302 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
303 |           add("(");
304 |         }
305 | 
306 |         add("function");
307 |         add(first);
308 | 
309 |         add(first.getNext());
310 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
311 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
312 | 
313 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
314 |           add(")");
315 |         }
316 |         break;
317 | 
318 |       case Token.GET:
319 |       case Token.SET:
320 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
323 | 
324 |         // Get methods are unnamed
325 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
326 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
327 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
328 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
329 |           add("get ");
330 |         } else {
331 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
332 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
333 |           add("set ");
334 |         }
335 | 
336 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
337 |         String name = n.getString();
338 |         Node fn = first;
339 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
340 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
341 | 
342 |         // Add the property name.
343 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
344 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
345 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
346 |             // unicode escaped.
347 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
348 |           add(name);
349 |         } else {
350 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
351 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
352 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
353 |             cc.addNumber(d);
354 |           } else {
355 |             addJsString(n.getString());
356 |           }
357 |         }
358 | 
359 |         add(parameters);
360 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
361 |         break;
362 | 
363 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
364 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
365 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
366 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
367 |         }
368 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
369 |         if (preserveBlock) {
370 |           cc.beginBlock();
371 |         }
372 | 
373 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
374 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
375 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
376 |                 !preserveBlock &&
377 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
378 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
379 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
380 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
381 | 
382 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
383 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
384 |             cc.endStatement();
385 |           }
386 | 
387 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
388 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
389 |           }
390 | 
391 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
392 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
393 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
394 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
395 |           }
396 |         }
397 |         if (preserveBlock) {
398 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
399 |         }
400 |         break;
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       case Token.FOR:
404 |         if (childCount == 4) {
405 |           add("for(");
406 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
407 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
408 |           } else {
409 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
410 |           }
411 |           add(";");
412 |           add(first.getNext());
413 |           add(";");
414 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         } else {
419 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
420 |           add("for(");
421 |           add(first);
422 |           add("in");
423 |           add(first.getNext());
424 |           add(")");
425 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
426 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
427 |         }
428 |         break;
429 | 
430 |       case Token.DO:
431 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
432 |         add("do");
433 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
434 |         add("while(");
435 |         add(last);
436 |         add(")");
437 |         cc.endStatement();
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.WHILE:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
442 |         add("while(");
443 |         add(first);
444 |         add(")");
445 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
446 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
447 |         break;
448 | 
449 |       case Token.EMPTY:
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
451 |         break;
452 | 
453 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(
455 |             childCount == 2,
456 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
457 |         Preconditions.checkState(
458 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
459 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
460 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
461 |         if (needsParens) {
462 |           add("(");
463 |         }
464 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
465 |         if (needsParens) {
466 |           add(")");
467 |         }
468 |         add(".");
469 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
470 |         break;
471 |       }
472 | 
473 |       case Token.GETELEM:
474 |         Preconditions.checkState(
475 |             childCount == 2,
476 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
477 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
478 |         add("[");
479 |         add(first.getNext());
480 |         add("]");
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.WITH:
484 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
485 |         add("with(");
486 |         add(first);
487 |         add(")");
488 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
489 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.INC:
493 |       case Token.DEC: {
494 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
495 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
496 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
497 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
498 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
499 |         if (postProp != 0) {
500 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
501 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
502 |         } else {
503 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
504 |           add(first);
505 |         }
506 |         break;
507 |       }
508 | 
509 |       case Token.CALL:
510 |         // We have two special cases here:
511 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
512 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
513 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
514 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
515 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
516 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
517 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
518 |         // that must be preserved.
519 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
520 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
521 |           add("(0,");
522 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
523 |           add(")");
524 |         } else {
525 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
526 |         }
527 |         add("(");
528 |         addList(first.getNext());
529 |         add(")");
530 |         break;
531 | 
532 |       case Token.IF:
533 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
534 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
535 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
536 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
537 |           cc.beginBlock();
538 |         }
539 | 
540 |         add("if(");
541 |         add(first);
542 |         add(")");
543 | 
544 |         if (hasElse) {
545 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
546 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
547 |           add("else");
548 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
549 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
550 |         } else {
551 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
552 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
553 |         }
554 | 
555 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
556 |           cc.endBlock();
557 |         }
558 |         break;
559 | 
560 |       case Token.NULL:
561 |       case Token.THIS:
562 |       case Token.FALSE:
563 |       case Token.TRUE:
564 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
565 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
566 |         break;
567 | 
568 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
569 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
570 |         add("continue");
571 |         if (childCount == 1) {
572 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
573 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
574 |           }
575 |           add(" ");
576 |           add(first);
577 |         }
578 |         cc.endStatement();
579 |         break;
580 | 
581 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
582 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
583 |         add("debugger");
584 |         cc.endStatement();
585 |         break;
586 | 
587 |       case Token.BREAK:
588 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
589 |         add("break");
590 |         if (childCount == 1) {
591 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
592 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
593 |           }
594 |           add(" ");
595 |           add(first);
596 |         }
597 |         cc.endStatement();
598 |         break;
599 | 
600 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
601 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n.getString());
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
726 |       // ignored for our purposes.
727 |       case Token.SETNAME:
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       default:
731 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
732 |     }
733 | 
734 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
735 |   }

===== M0550 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getCount(Node,Predicate<Node>,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2797-2812
Documentation:
/**
   * @return The number of times the the predicate is true for the node
   * or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2797 |   static int getCount(
2798 |       Node n, Predicate<Node> pred, Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2799 |     int total = 0;
2800 | 
2801 |     if (pred.apply(n)) {
2802 |       total++;
2803 |     }
2804 | 
2805 |     if (traverseChildrenPred.apply(n)) {
2806 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2807 |         total += getCount(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred);
2808 |       }
2809 |     }
2810 | 
2811 |     return total;
2812 |   }

===== M0554 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getInfoForNameNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2929-2943
Documentation:
/**
   * @param nameNode A name node
   * @return The JSDocInfo for the name node
   */
Method code:
2929 |   static JSDocInfo getInfoForNameNode(Node nameNode) {
2930 |     JSDocInfo info = null;
2931 |     Node parent = null;
2932 |     if (nameNode != null) {
2933 |       info = nameNode.getJSDocInfo();
2934 |       parent = nameNode.getParent();
2935 |     }
2936 | 
2937 |     if (info == null && parent != null &&
2938 |         ((parent.getType() == Token.VAR && parent.hasOneChild()) ||
2939 |           parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION)) {

===== M0555 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.classifyCallSite(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 351-391
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine which, if any, of the supported types the call site is.
   */
Method code:
351 |   private CallSiteType classifyCallSite(Node callNode) {
352 |     Node parent = callNode.getParent();
353 |     Node grandParent = parent.getParent();
354 | 
355 |     // Verify the call site:
356 |     if (NodeUtil.isExprCall(parent)) {
357 |       // This is a simple call?  Example: "foo();".
358 |       return CallSiteType.SIMPLE_CALL;
359 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isExprAssign(grandParent)
360 |         && !NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(callNode, parent)
361 |         && parent.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME
362 |         && !NodeUtil.isConstantName(parent.getFirstChild())) {
363 |       // This is a simple assignment.  Example: "x = foo();"
364 |       return CallSiteType.SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT;
365 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME
366 |         && !NodeUtil.isConstantName(parent)
367 |         && grandParent.getType() == Token.VAR
368 |         && grandParent.hasOneChild()) {
369 |       // This is a var declaration.  Example: "var x = foo();"
370 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Should we be checking for constants on the
371 |       // left-hand-side of the assignments (and handling them as EXPRESSION?
372 |       return CallSiteType.VAR_DECL_SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT;
373 |     } else {
374 |       Node expressionRoot = ExpressionDecomposer.findExpressionRoot(callNode);
375 |       if (expressionRoot != null) {
376 |         ExpressionDecomposer decomposer = new ExpressionDecomposer(
377 |             compiler, safeNameIdSupplier, knownConstants);
378 |         DecompositionType type = decomposer.canExposeExpression(
379 |             callNode);
380 |         if (type == DecompositionType.MOVABLE) {
381 |           return CallSiteType.EXPRESSION;
382 |         } else if (type == DecompositionType.DECOMPOSABLE) {
383 |           return CallSiteType.DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION;
384 |         } else {
385 |           Preconditions.checkState(type == DecompositionType.UNDECOMPOSABLE);
386 |         }
387 |       }
388 |     }
389 | 
390 |     return CallSiteType.UNSUPPORTED;
391 |   }

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addNonEmptyStatement(Node,Context,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 777-833
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a block or expression, substituting a VOID with an empty statement.
   * This is used for "for (...);" and "if (...);" type statements.
   *
   * @param n The node to print.
   * @param context The context to determine how the node should be printed.
   */
Method code:
777 |   private void addNonEmptyStatement(
778 |       Node n, Context context, boolean allowNonBlockChild) {
779 |     Node nodeToProcess = n;
780 | 
781 |     if (!allowNonBlockChild && n.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
782 |       throw new Error("Missing BLOCK child.");
783 |     }
784 | 
785 |     // Strip unneeded blocks, that is blocks with <2 children unless
786 |     // the CodePrinter specifically wants to keep them.
787 |     if (n.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
788 |       int count = getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2);
789 |       if (count == 0) {
790 |         if (cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks()) {
791 |           cc.beginBlock();
792 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
793 |         } else {
794 |           cc.endStatement(true);
795 |         }
796 |         return;
797 |       }
798 | 
799 |       if (count == 1) {
800 |         // Hack around a couple of browser bugs:
801 |         //   Safari needs a block around function declarations.
802 |         //   IE6/7 needs a block around DOs.
803 |         Node firstAndOnlyChild = getFirstNonEmptyChild(n);
804 |         boolean alwaysWrapInBlock = cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks();
805 |         if (alwaysWrapInBlock || isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(firstAndOnlyChild)) {
806 |           cc.beginBlock();
807 |           add(firstAndOnlyChild, Context.STATEMENT);
808 |           cc.maybeLineBreak();
809 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
810 |           return;
811 |         } else {
812 |           // Continue with the only child.
813 |           nodeToProcess = firstAndOnlyChild;
814 |         }
815 |       }
816 | 
817 |       if (count > 1) {
818 |         context = Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
819 |       }
820 |     }
821 | 
822 |     if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
823 |       cc.endStatement(true);
824 |     } else {
825 |       add(nodeToProcess, context);
826 | 
827 |       // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions - so any
828 |       // VAR in a statement context needs a call to endStatement() here.
829 |       if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.VAR) {
830 |         cc.endStatement();
831 |       }
832 |     }
833 |   }

===== M0576 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 36-64
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
36 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
37 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
38 |     // can be simplified.
39 | 
40 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
41 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
42 |     if (parent == null) {
43 |       return null;
44 |     }
45 | 
46 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
47 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
48 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isFunction(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
49 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
50 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
51 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
52 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
53 |       }
54 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isAssign(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
55 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
56 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
57 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
58 |           isExtern);
59 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LP) {
60 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
61 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
62 |     }
63 |     return null;
64 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType,Node)

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
364 |   boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
365 |     if (prototype == null) {
366 |       return false;
367 |     }
368 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
369 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
370 |       return false;

374 |         ? null : (PrototypeObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
375 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
376 | 
377 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
378 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
379 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
380 | 
381 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
382 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
383 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
384 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
385 |     }
386 |

388 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
389 |       if (superClass != null) {
390 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
391 |       }
392 | 
393 |       if (isInterface()) {

404 |     }

406 |     return true;

===== M0593 =====

   * Gather any names declared in the local scope.
   */
Method code:
472 |   private static void gatherLocalNames(Node n, Set<String> names) {
473 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
474 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
475 |         names.add(n.getFirstChild().getString());

484 |       }
485 |     }

488 |       gatherLocalNames(c, names);

===== M0600 =====

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 349-378
Documentation:
/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
349 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
350 |       Preconditions.checkState(
351 |           n.getType() == Token.NAME
352 |           || n.getType() == Token.STRING
353 |           || n.getType() == Token.GET
354 |           || n.getType() == Token.SET);
355 | 
356 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
357 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
358 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
359 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);
360 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
361 |           (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
362 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
363 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME || isProperty) {
364 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
365 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
366 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
367 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
368 |           if (assertOnChange) {
369 |             String name = n.getString();
370 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
371 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
372 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
373 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
374 |           }
375 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
376 |         }
377 |       }
378 |     }

===== M0601 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 495-542
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
495 |     private void extractForInitializer(
496 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
497 | 
498 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
499 |         next = c.getNext();
500 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
501 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
502 |         switch (c.getType()) {
503 |           case Token.LABEL:
504 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
505 |             break;
506 |           case Token.FOR:
507 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
508 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
509 |               if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
510 |                 // Transform:
511 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
512 |                 // to:
513 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
514 |                 Node newStatement = first;
515 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
516 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
517 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
518 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
519 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
520 |               }
521 |             } else if (c.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
522 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
523 |               Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
524 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
525 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
526 | 
527 |               Node newStatement;
528 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
529 |               // but are handled differently.
530 |               if (init.getType() == Token.VAR) {
531 |                 newStatement = init;
532 |               } else {
533 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
534 |               }
535 | 
536 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
537 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
538 |             }
539 |             break;
540 |         }
541 |       }
542 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.ScriptRuntime.getMessage(String,Object[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java

Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException
472 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);

   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.

===== M0638 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.isCandidateFunction(Function)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 335-361
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks if the given function matches the criteria for an inlinable
   * function.
   */
Method code:
335 |   private boolean isCandidateFunction(Function fn) {
336 |     // Don't inline exported functions.
337 |     String fnName = fn.getName();
338 |     if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(fnName)) {
339 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Should we allow internal references to be inlined?
340 |       // An exported name can be replaced externally, any inlined instance
341 |       // would not reflect this change.
342 |       // To allow inlining we need to be able to distinguish between exports
343 |       // that are used in a read-only fashion and those that can be replaced
344 |       // by external definitions.
345 |       return false;
346 |     }
347 | 
348 |     // Don't inline this special function
349 |     if (RenameProperties.RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(fnName)) {
350 |       return false;
351 |     }
352 | 
353 |     // Don't inline if we are specializing and the function can't be fixed up
354 |     if (specializationState != null &&
355 |         !specializationState.canFixupFunction(fn.getFunctionNode())) {
356 |       return false;
357 |     }
358 | 
359 |     Node fnNode = fn.getFunctionNode();
360 |     return injector.doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(fnName, fnNode);
361 |   }

===== M0642 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.doesLowerCost(Node,int,int,int,int,int,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 791-819
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether inlining will lower cost.
   */
Method code:
791 |   private boolean doesLowerCost(
792 |       Node fnNode, int callCost,
793 |       int directInlines, int costDeltaDirect,
794 |       int blockInlines, int costDeltaBlock,
795 |       boolean removable) {
796 | 
797 |     // Determine the threshold value for this inequality:
798 |     //     inline_cost < call_cost
799 |     // But solve it for the function declaration size so the size of it
800 |     // is only calculated once and terminated early if possible.
801 | 
802 |     int fnInstanceCount = directInlines + blockInlines - (removable ? 1 : 0);
803 |     // Prevent division by zero.
804 |     if (fnInstanceCount == 0) {
805 |       // Special case single reference function that are being block inlined:
806 |       // If the cost of the inline is greater than the function definition size,
807 |       // don't inline.
808 |       if (blockInlines > 0 && costDeltaBlock > 0) {
809 |         return false;
810 |       }
811 |       return true;
812 |     }
813 | 
814 |     int costDelta = (directInlines * costDeltaDirect) +
815 |         (blockInlines * costDeltaBlock);
816 |     int threshold = (callCost - costDelta) / fnInstanceCount;
817 | 
818 |     return InlineCostEstimator.getCost(fnNode, threshold + 1) <= threshold;
819 |   }

   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.

Method code:
2077 |   public Node cloneTree() {
2078 |     Node result = cloneNode();

===== M0649 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidatesReferences.maybeAddReference(NodeTraversal,FunctionState,Node,JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 490-515
Documentation:

Method code:
490 |     void maybeAddReference(NodeTraversal t, FunctionState fs,
491 |         Node callNode, JSModule module) {
492 |       if (!fs.canInline()) {
493 |         return;
494 |       }
495 | 
496 |       boolean referenceAdded = false;
497 |       InliningMode mode = fs.canInlineDirectly()
498 |            ? InliningMode.DIRECT : InliningMode.BLOCK;
499 |       referenceAdded = maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(
500 |           t, fs, callNode, module, mode);
501 |       if (!referenceAdded &&
502 |           mode == InliningMode.DIRECT && blockFunctionInliningEnabled) {
503 |         // This reference can not be directly inlined, see if
504 |         // block replacement inlining is possible.
505 |         mode = InliningMode.BLOCK;
506 |         referenceAdded = maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(
507 |             t, fs, callNode, module, mode);
508 |       }
509 | 
510 |       if (!referenceAdded) {
511 |         // Don't try to remove a function if we can't inline all
512 |         // the references.
513 |         fs.setRemove(false);
514 |       }
515 |     }

934 |     @Override
935 |     Node processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression exprNode) {
936 |       int type = transformTokenType(exprNode.getType());
937 |       Node operand = transform(exprNode.getOperand());
938 |       if (type == Token.NEG && operand.getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
939 |         operand.setDouble(-operand.getDouble());

949 |               msg,

953 |           if (!validAssignmentTarget(operand)) {

===== M0670 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1161-1233
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1161 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1162 |     switch (type) {
1163 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1176 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1177 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1178 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1179 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1180 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1181 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1182 |       case Token.EQ:
1183 |       case Token.NE:
1184 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1185 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1186 |       case Token.LT:
1187 |       case Token.GT:
1188 |       case Token.LE:
1189 |       case Token.GE:
1190 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1191 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1192 |       case Token.LSH:
1193 |       case Token.RSH:
1194 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1195 |       case Token.SUB:
1196 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1197 |       case Token.MUL:
1198 |       case Token.MOD:
1199 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1200 |       case Token.INC:
1201 |       case Token.DEC:
1202 |       case Token.NEW:
1203 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1204 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1205 |       case Token.VOID:
1206 |       case Token.NOT:
1207 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1208 |       case Token.POS:
1209 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1210 | 
1211 |       case Token.CALL:
1212 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1213 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1214 |       // Data values
1215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1216 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1217 |       case Token.FALSE:
1218 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1219 |       case Token.NAME:
1220 |       case Token.NULL:
1221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1222 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1223 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1224 |       case Token.STRING:
1225 |       case Token.THIS:
1226 |       case Token.TRUE:
1227 |         return 15;
1228 | 
1229 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1230 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1231 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1232 |     }
1233 |   }

===== M0672 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1332-1340
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1332 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1333 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1334 |       return -1;
1335 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1336 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1337 |     } else {
1338 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1339 |     }
1340 |   }

Original range: 433-453

Method code:
433 |     @Override
434 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
435 |       if (lineBreak) {
436 |         if (sawFunction) {
437 |           startNewLine();
438 |           sawFunction = false;
439 |         }
440 |       }
441 | 
442 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
443 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
444 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
445 |       int len = code.length();
446 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
447 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
448 |         if (ch == ';') {
449 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |       maybeCutLine();
453 |     }

===== M0675 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 576-608
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
576 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
577 |       Preconditions.checkState(
578 |           functionBody.getParent().getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
579 |       Node previous = null;
580 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
581 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
582 |       // are already in the right place.
583 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
584 |         previous = current;
585 |         current = current.getNext();
586 |       }
587 | 
588 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
589 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
590 |       while (current != null) {
591 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
592 |         Node next = current.getNext();
593 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
594 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
595 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
596 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
597 | 
598 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
599 |           // previous declarations).
600 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
601 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
602 |         } else {
603 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
604 |           previous = current;
605 |         }
606 |         current = next;
607 |       }
608 |     }

/**
   * A post-order traversal, calling Vistor.visit for each child matching
   * the predicate.
   */
Method code:
2842 |   static void visitPostOrder(Node node,
2843 |                      Visitor vistor,
2844 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2845 |     if (traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2846 |       for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2847 |         visitPostOrder(c, vistor, traverseChildrenPred);
2848 |       }
2849 |     }
2850 | 
2851 |     vistor.visit(node);
2852 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 155-186
Documentation:

Method code:
155 |   @Override
156 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
157 |     switch (n.getType()) {
158 |       case Token.NAME:
159 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
160 |         if (newName != null) {
161 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
162 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
163 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
164 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
165 |           }
166 |           n.setString(newName);
167 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
168 |         }
169 |         break;
170 | 
171 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
172 |         // Remove the function body scope
173 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
174 |         nameStack.pop();
175 |         break;
176 | 
177 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
178 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
179 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
180 | 
181 |       case Token.CATCH:
182 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
183 |         nameStack.pop();
184 |         break;
185 |     }
186 |   }

===== M0694 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/TokenStream.java
Original range: 314-339
Documentation:

Method code:
314 |     public static boolean isJSIdentifier(String s) {
315 |         int length = s.length();
316 | 
317 |         if (length == 0 || !Character.isJavaIdentifierStart(s.charAt(0)))
318 |             return false;
319 | 
320 |         for (int i=1; i<length; i++) {
321 |             char c = s.charAt(i);
322 |             if (!Character.isJavaIdentifierPart(c)) {
323 |                 if (c == '\\') {
324 |                     if (! ((i + 5) < length)
325 |                         && (s.charAt(i + 1) == 'u')
326 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 2), 0)
327 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 3), 0)
328 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 4), 0)
329 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 5), 0)) {
330 |                         return true;
331 |                      }
332 |                 }
333 | 
334 |                 return false;
335 |             }
336 |         }
337 | 
338 |         return true;
339 |     }

===== M0718 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.fixUnitializedVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 150-171
Documentation:
/**
   *  For all VAR node with uninitialized declarations, set
   *  the values to be "undefined".
   */
Method code:
150 |   private void fixUnitializedVarDeclarations(Node n) {
151 |     // Inner loop structure must already have logic to initialize its
152 |     // variables.  In particular FOR-IN structures must not be modified.
153 |     if (NodeUtil.isLoopStructure(n)) {
154 |       return;
155 |     }
156 | 
157 |     // For all VARs
158 |     if (NodeUtil.isVar(n)) {
159 |       Node name = n.getFirstChild();
160 |       // It isn't initialized.
161 |       if (!name.hasChildren()) {
162 |         Node srcLocation = name;
163 |         name.addChildToBack(NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation));
164 |       }
165 |       return;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
169 |       fixUnitializedVarDeclarations(c);
170 |     }
171 |   }

===== M0719 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 396-409
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
396 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
397 |       Node ret;
398 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
399 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
400 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
401 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
402 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
403 |       } else {
404 |         ret = transform(n);
405 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
406 |       }
407 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
408 |       return ret;
409 |     }

===== M0722 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.findNonconditionalParent(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 253-270
Documentation:
/**
   * @return "expression" or the node closest to "expression", that does not
   * have a conditional ancestor.
   */
Method code:
253 |   private static Node findNonconditionalParent(
254 |       Node subExpression, Node expressionRoot) {
255 |      Node result = subExpression;
256 | 
257 |      for (Node child = subExpression, parent = child.getParent();
258 |           parent != expressionRoot;
259 |           child = parent, parent = child.getParent()) {
260 |        if (isConditionalOp(parent)) {
261 |          // Only the first child is always executed, if the function may never
262 |          // be called, don't inline it.
263 |          if (child != parent.getFirstChild()) {
264 |            result = parent;
265 |          }
266 |        }
267 |      }
268 | 
269 |      return result;
270 |   }

/**
   * @return For the subExpression, find the nearest statement Node before which
   * it can be inlined.  Null if no such location can be found.
   */
Method code:
605 |   static Node findInjectionPoint(Node subExpression) {
606 |     Node expressionRoot = findExpressionRoot(subExpression);
607 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(expressionRoot);
608 |

610 |

===== M0739 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M0743 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.resolveInlineConflictsForFunction(FunctionState)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 744-770
Documentation:
/**
   * @see #resolveInlineConflicts
   */
Method code:
744 |   private void resolveInlineConflictsForFunction(FunctionState fs) {
745 |     // Functions that aren't referenced don't cause conflicts.
746 |     if (!fs.hasReferences()) {
747 |       return;
748 |     }
749 | 
750 |     Node fnNode = fs.getFn().getFunctionNode();
751 |     Set<String> names = findCalledFunctions(fnNode);
752 |     if (!names.isEmpty()) {
753 |       // Prevent the removal of the referenced functions.
754 |       for (String name : names) {
755 |         FunctionState fsCalled = fns.get(name);
756 |         if (fsCalled != null && fsCalled.canRemove()) {
757 |           fsCalled.setRemove(false);
758 |           // For functions that can no longer be removed, check if they should
759 |           // still be inlined.
760 |           if (!mimimizeCost(fsCalled)) {
761 |             // It can't be inlined remove it from the list.
762 |             fsCalled.setInline(false);
763 |           }
764 |         }
765 |       }
766 | 
767 |       // Make a copy of the Node, so it isn't changed by other inlines.
768 |       fs.setSafeFnNode(fs.getFn().getFunctionNode().cloneTree());
769 |     }
770 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java

Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
986 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
987 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
988 |     if (callNode.getType() != Token.CALL) {
989 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
990 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
991 |     }
992 | 
993 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
994 |       return false;
995 |     }
996 | 
997 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
998 | 
999 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
1000 |     if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME) {
1001 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
1002 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
1003 |         return false;
1004 |       }
1005 |     } else if (nameNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
1006 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
1007 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
1008 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1009 |         return false;
1010 |       }
1011 | 
1012 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
1013 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
1014 |         return false;
1015 |       }
1016 | 
1017 |       // Math.floor has no sideeffects.
1018 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This is a terrible terrible hack, until
1019 |       // I create a definitionprovider that understands namespacing.
1020 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME) {
1021 |         if ("Math.floor".equals(nameNode.getQualifiedName())) {
1022 |           return false;
1023 |         }
1024 |       }
1025 | 
1026 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
1027 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.REGEXP
1028 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1029 |           return false;
1030 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.STRING
1031 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
1032 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1033 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
1034 |           if (param != null &&
1035 |               (param.getType() == Token.STRING
1036 |                   || param.getType() == Token.REGEXP))
1037 |           return false;
1038 |         }
1039 |       }
1040 |     }
1041 | 
1042 |     return true;
1043 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 206-223
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
206 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
207 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
208 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
209 |     if (parent == null
210 |         || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION
211 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
212 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
213 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
214 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
215 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
216 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
217 |       }
218 | 
219 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
220 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
221 |       }
222 |     }
223 |   }

     * For the Var declared in the current scope determine if it is possible
     * to revert the name to its orginal form without conflicting with other
     * values.

335 |     void handleScopeVar(Var v) {
336 |       String name  = v.getName();

112 |   @Override
113 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
114 |     Preconditions.checkState(compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized());
115 | 
116 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, new FindCandidateFunctions());
117 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
118 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
119 |     }

121 |        new FindCandidatesReferences(fns, anonFns));

125 |     }

128 |     // prevent function names from being moved into temporaries during

130 |     // the Inline callback from finding the function calls.
131 |     //
132 |     // This pass already assumes these are constants, so this is safe for anyone
133 |     // using function inlining.

   * duplicates.

429 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
430 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
431 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

85 |     try {
86 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
87 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
88 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
89 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
90 |           logger_);

93 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));

===== M0799 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 552-570
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
552 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
553 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
554 |         next = c.getNext();
555 |         if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
556 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
557 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
558 |           }
559 | 
560 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
561 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
562 |             c.removeChild(name);
563 |             Node newVar = new Node(
564 |                 Token.VAR, name, n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
565 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
566 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
567 |           }
568 |         }
569 |       }
570 |     }

===== M0802 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.trimCanidatesUsingOnCost()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 665-682
Documentation:
/**
   * Remove entries from the list of candidates that can't be inlined.
   */
Method code:
665 |   void trimCanidatesUsingOnCost() {
666 |     Iterator<Entry<String, FunctionState>> i;
667 |     for (i = fns.entrySet().iterator(); i.hasNext();) {
668 |       FunctionState fs = i.next().getValue();
669 |       if (fs.hasReferences()) {
670 |         // Only inline function if it decreases the code size.
671 |         boolean lowersCost = mimimizeCost(fs);
672 |         if (!lowersCost) {
673 |           // It shouldn't be inlined; remove it from the list.
674 |           i.remove();
675 |         }
676 |       } else if (!fs.canRemove()) {
677 |         // Don't bother tracking functions without references that can't be
678 |         // removed.
679 |         i.remove();
680 |       }
681 |     }
682 |   }

===== M0807 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1988-1990
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1988 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1989 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1990 |   }

===== M0808 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

===== M0809 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
961 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
962 |     int count = 0;
963 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
964 |       count++;
965 |     }
966 | 
967 |     int[] keys = new int[count];
968 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
969 |       count--;
970 |       keys[count] = x.getType();
971 |     }
972 | 
973 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
974 |     return keys;
975 |   }

===== M0814 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 231-279
Documentation:

Method code:
231 |     @Override
232 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
233 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
234 |         String name = n.getString();
235 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
236 |           return;
237 |         }
238 | 
239 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
240 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
241 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
242 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
243 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
244 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
245 |             expectedConst = true;
246 |           } else {
247 |             expectedConst = false;
248 | 
249 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
250 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
251 |             if (var != null) {
252 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
253 |             }
254 | 
255 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
256 |               expectedConst = true;
257 |             } else {
258 |               expectedConst = false;
259 |             }
260 |           }
261 | 
262 |           if (expectedConst) {
263 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
264 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
265 |           } else {
266 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
267 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
268 |           }
269 |         }
270 | 
271 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
272 |         if (value == null) {
273 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
274 |         } else {
275 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
276 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
277 |         }
278 |       }
279 |     }

===== M0822 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 113-153
Documentation:

Method code:
113 |   @Override
114 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
115 | 
116 |     switch (n.getType()) {
117 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
118 |         {
119 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
120 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
121 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
122 | 
123 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
124 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
125 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
126 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
127 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
128 |           }
129 | 
130 | 
131 | 
132 |           // Add the function parameters
133 | 
134 |           // Add the function body declarations
135 | 
136 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
137 |         }
138 |         break;
139 | 
140 |       case Token.CATCH:
141 |         {
142 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
143 | 
144 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
145 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
146 | 
147 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |     }
151 | 
152 |     return true;
153 |   }

===== M0829 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }
