Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 234-256
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

===== M0846 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.isCandidateUsage(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 427-461
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the name is used in a way that might be a candidate
   *   for inlining.
   */
Method code:
427 |   static boolean isCandidateUsage(Node name) {
428 |     Node parent = name.getParent();
429 |     Preconditions.checkState(name.getType() == Token.NAME);
430 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
431 |       // This is a declaration.  Duplicate declarations are handle during
432 |       // function candidate gathering.
433 |       return true;
434 |     }
435 | 
436 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.CALL && parent.getFirstChild() == name) {
437 |       // This is a normal reference to the function.
438 |       return true;
439 |     }
440 | 
441 |     // Check for a ".call" to the named function:
442 |     //   CALL
443 |     //     GETPROP/GETELEM
444 |     //       NAME
445 |     //       STRING == "call"
446 |     //     This-Value
447 |     //     Function-parameter-1
448 |     //     ...
449 |     if (NodeUtil.isGet(parent)
450 |          && name == parent.getFirstChild()
451 |          && name.getNext().getType() == Token.STRING
452 |          && name.getNext().getString().equals("call")) {
453 |       Node gramps = name.getAncestor(2);
454 |       if (gramps.getType() == Token.CALL
455 |           && gramps.getFirstChild() == parent) {
456 |         // Yep, a ".call".
457 |         return true;
458 |       }
459 |     }
460 |     return false;
461 |   }

===== M0854 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidateFunctions.findNamedFunctions(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 175-204
Documentation:

Method code:
175 |     public void findNamedFunctions(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
176 |       if (!NodeUtil.isStatement(n)) {
177 |         // There aren't any interesting functions here.
178 |         return;
179 |       }
180 | 
181 |       switch (n.getType()) {
182 |         // Functions expressions in the form of:
183 |         //   var fooFn = function(x) { return ... }
184 |         case Token.VAR:
185 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
186 |           Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
187 |           if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME && nameNode.hasChildren()
188 |               && nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
189 |             maybeAddFunction(new FunctionVar(n), t.getModule());
190 |           }
191 |           break;
192 | 
193 |         // Named functions
194 |         // function Foo(x) { return ... }
195 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)
197 |               || parent.getType() == Token.LABEL);
198 |           if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
199 |             Function fn = new NamedFunction(n);
200 |             maybeAddFunction(fn, t.getModule());
201 |           }
202 |           break;
203 |       }
204 |     }

===== M0863 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.extractExpression(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 440-509
Documentation:
/**
   * @param expr The expression to extract.
   * @param injectionPoint The node before which to added the extracted
   *     expression.
   * @return The extract statement node.
   */
Method code:
440 |   private Node extractExpression(Node expr, Node injectionPoint) {
441 |     Node parent = expr.getParent();
442 | 
443 |     boolean isLhsOfAssignOp = NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent)
444 |         && !NodeUtil.isAssign(parent)
445 |         && parent.getFirstChild() == expr;
446 | 
447 |     Node firstExtractedNode = null;
448 | 
449 |     // Expressions on the LHS of an assignment-op must have any possible
450 |     // side-effects extracted as the value must be duplicated:
451 |     //    next().foo += 2;
452 |     // becomes:
453 |     //    var t1 = next();
454 |     //    t1.foo = t1.foo + 2;
455 |     if (isLhsOfAssignOp && NodeUtil.isGet(expr)) {
456 |       for (Node n : expr.children()) {
457 |         if (n.getType() != Token.STRING && !isConstantName(n, knownConstants)) {
458 |           Node extractedNode = extractExpression(n, injectionPoint);
459 |           if (firstExtractedNode == null) {
460 |             firstExtractedNode = extractedNode;
461 |           }
462 |         }
463 |       }
464 |     }
465 | 
466 |     // The temp is known to be constant.
467 |     String tempName = getTempConstantValueName();
468 |     Node replacementValueNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, tempName)
469 |         .copyInformationFrom(expr);
470 | 
471 |     Node tempNameValue;
472 | 
473 |     // If it is ASSIGN_XXX, keep the assignment in place and extract the
474 |     // original value of the LHS operand.
475 |     if (isLhsOfAssignOp) {
476 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isName(expr) || NodeUtil.isGet(expr));
477 |       // Transform "x += 2" into "x = temp + 2"
478 |       Node opNode = new Node(NodeUtil.getOpFromAssignmentOp(parent))
479 |           .copyInformationFrom(parent);
480 | 
481 |       Node rightOperand = parent.getLastChild();
482 | 
483 |       parent.setType(Token.ASSIGN);
484 |       parent.replaceChild(rightOperand, opNode);
485 |       opNode.addChildToFront(replacementValueNode);
486 |       opNode.addChildToBack(rightOperand);
487 | 
488 |       // The original expression is still being used, so make a clone.
489 |       tempNameValue = expr.cloneTree();
490 |     } else {
491 |       // Replace the expression with the temporary name.
492 |       parent.replaceChild(expr, replacementValueNode);
493 | 
494 |       // Keep the original node so that CALL expressions can still be found
495 |       // and inlined properly.
496 |       tempNameValue = expr;
497 |     }
498 | 
499 |     // Re-add the expression in the declaration of the temporary name.
500 |     Node tempVarNode = NodeUtil.newVarNode(tempName, tempNameValue);
501 | 
502 |     Node injectionPointParent = injectionPoint.getParent();
503 |     injectionPointParent.addChildBefore(tempVarNode, injectionPoint);
504 | 
505 |     if (firstExtractedNode == null) {
506 |       firstExtractedNode = tempVarNode;
507 |     }
508 |     return firstExtractedNode;
509 |   }

===== M0867 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

===== M0871 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(Node,Node,Supplier<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 112-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Get a mapping for function parameter names to call arguments.
   */
Method code:
112 |   static LinkedHashMap<String, Node> getFunctionCallParameterMap(
113 |       Node fnNode, Node callNode, Supplier<String> safeNameIdSupplier) {
114 |     // Create an argName -> expression map
115 |     // NOTE: A linked map is created here to provide ordering.
116 |     LinkedHashMap<String, Node> argMap = Maps.newLinkedHashMap();
117 | 
118 |     // CALL NODE: [ NAME, ARG1, ARG2, ... ]
119 |     Node cArg = callNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
120 |     if (cArg != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
121 |       argMap.put(THIS_MARKER, cArg);
122 |       cArg = cArg.getNext();
123 |     } else {
124 |       // 'apply' isn't supported yet.
125 |       Preconditions.checkState(!NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectApply(callNode));
126 |       argMap.put(THIS_MARKER, NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(callNode));
127 |     }
128 | 
129 |     for (Node fnArg : NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode).children()) {
130 |       if (cArg != null) {
131 |         argMap.put(fnArg.getString(), cArg);
132 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
133 |       } else {
134 |         Node srcLocation = callNode;
135 |         argMap.put(fnArg.getString(), NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation));
136 |       }
137 |     }
138 | 
139 |     // Add temp names for arguments that don't have named parameters in the
140 |     // called function.
141 |     int anonArg = 0;
142 |     while (cArg != null) {
143 |       String uniquePlaceholder =
144 |         getUniqueAnonymousParameterName(safeNameIdSupplier);
145 |       argMap.put(uniquePlaceholder, cArg);
146 |       cArg = cArg.getNext();
147 |     }
148 | 
149 |     return argMap;
150 |   }

===== M0873 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.decomposeSubExpressions(Node,Node,DecompositionState)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 300-325
Documentation:
/**
   * @param n The node with which to start iterating.
   * @param stopNode A node after which to stop iterating.
   */
Method code:
300 |   private void decomposeSubExpressions(
301 |       Node n, Node stopNode, DecompositionState state) {
302 |     if (n == null || n == stopNode) {
303 |       return;
304 |     }
305 | 
306 |     // Never try to decompose an object literal key.
307 |     Preconditions.checkState(!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent()));
308 | 
309 |     // Decompose the children in reverse evaluation order.  This simplifies
310 |     // determining if the any of the children following have side-effects.
311 |     // If they do we need to be more aggressive about removing values
312 |     // from the expression.
313 |     decomposeSubExpressions(
314 |         n.getNext(), stopNode, state);
315 | 
316 |     // Now this node.
317 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Move "safety" code to a shared class.
318 |     if (isExpressionTreeUnsafe(n, state.sideEffects)) {
319 |       // Either there were preexisting side-effects, or this node has
320 |       // side-effects.
321 |       state.sideEffects = true;
322 |       state.extractBeforeStatement = extractExpression(
323 |           n, state.extractBeforeStatement);
324 |     }
325 |   }

/**
     * Limit the number of special cases where LABELs need to be handled. Only
     * BLOCK and loops are allowed to be labeled.  Loop labels must remain in
     * place as the named continues are not allowed for labeled blocks.
     */
Method code:
463 |     private void normalizeLabels(Node n) {
464 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(n.getType() == Token.LABEL);
465 | 
466 |       Node last = n.getLastChild();
467 |       switch (last.getType()) {
468 |         case Token.LABEL:

471 |         case Token.WHILE:

479 |           reportCodeChange("LABEL normalization");
480 |           return;

===== M0881 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inlineCostDelta(Node,Set<String>,InliningMode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 850-901
Documentation:
/**
   * @return The difference between the function definition cost and
   *     inline cost.
   */
Method code:
850 |   private static int inlineCostDelta(
851 |       Node fnNode, Set<String> namesToAlias, InliningMode mode) {
852 |     // The part of the function that is never inlined:
853 |     //    "function xx(xx,xx){}" (15 + (param count * 3) -1;
854 |     int paramCount = NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode).getChildCount();
855 |     int commaCount = (paramCount > 1) ? paramCount - 1 : 0;
856 |     int costDeltaFunctionOverhead = 15 + commaCount +
857 |         (paramCount * InlineCostEstimator.ESTIMATED_IDENTIFIER_COST);
858 | 
859 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
860 |     if (!block.hasChildren()) {
861 |       // Assume the inline cost is zero for empty functions.
862 |       return -costDeltaFunctionOverhead;
863 |     }
864 | 
865 |     if (mode == InliningMode.DIRECT) {
866 |       // The part of the function that is inlined using direct inlining:
867 |       //    "return " (7)
868 |       return -(costDeltaFunctionOverhead + 7);
869 |     } else {
870 |       int aliasCount = namesToAlias.size();
871 | 
872 |       // Originally, we estimated purely base on the function code size, relying
873 |       // on later optimizations. But that did not produce good results, so here
874 |       // we try to estimate the something closer to the actual inlined coded.
875 | 
876 |       // NOTE 1: Result overhead is only if there is an assignment, but
877 |       // getting that information would require some refactoring.
878 |       // NOTE 2: The aliasing overhead is currently an under-estimate,
879 |       // as some parameters are aliased because of the parameters used.
880 |       // Perhaps we should just assume all parameters will be aliased?
881 |       final int INLINE_BLOCK_OVERHEAD = 4; // "X:{}"
882 |       final int PER_RETURN_OVERHEAD = 2;   // "return" --> "break X"
883 |       final int PER_RETURN_RESULT_OVERHEAD = 3; // "XX="
884 |       final int PER_ALIAS_OVERHEAD = 3; // "XX="
885 | 
886 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Counting the number of returns is relatively expensive
887 |       //   this information should be determined during the traversal and
888 |       //   cached.
889 |       int returnCount = NodeUtil.getNodeTypeReferenceCount(
890 |           block, Token.RETURN, new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
891 |       int resultCount = (returnCount > 0) ? returnCount - 1 : 0;
892 |       int baseOverhead = (returnCount > 0) ? INLINE_BLOCK_OVERHEAD : 0;
893 | 
894 |       int overhead = baseOverhead
895 |           + returnCount * PER_RETURN_OVERHEAD
896 |           + resultCount * PER_RETURN_RESULT_OVERHEAD
897 |           + aliasCount * PER_ALIAS_OVERHEAD;
898 | 
899 |       return (overhead - costDeltaFunctionOverhead);
900 |     }
901 |   }

===== M0890 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 330-357
Documentation:

Method code:
330 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
331 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
332 |     // two cases:
333 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
334 |     // and
335 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
336 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
337 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
338 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
339 |     //
340 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
341 |     //
342 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
343 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
344 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
345 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
346 |     //
347 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
348 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
349 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
350 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
351 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
352 | 
353 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
354 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
355 |     }
356 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType, propertyNode);
357 |   }

===== M0894 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 457-472
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
457 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
458 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
459 |       Set<String> directives = null;
460 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
461 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
462 |         if (directives == null) {
463 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
464 |         } else {
465 |           directives.add(directive);
466 |         }
467 |       }
468 | 
469 |       if (directives != null) {
470 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
471 |       }
472 |     }

===== M0896 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 118-149
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   @Override
119 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
120 |     new NodeTraversal(
121 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
122 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
123 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
124 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
125 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
126 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
127 |     }
128 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
129 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
130 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
131 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
132 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
133 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
134 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
135 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
136 |     //   function f() {
137 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
138 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
139 |     //   }
140 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
141 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
142 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
143 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
144 |         .process(externs, root);
145 | 
146 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
147 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
148 |     }
149 |   }

===== M0907 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.maybeAddTempsForCallArguments(Node,Map<String, Node>,Set<String>,CodingConvention)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 245-314
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the set of parameter names in set unsafe to include any
   * arguments from the call site that require aliases.
   * @param fnNode The FUNCTION node to be inlined.
   * @param argMap The argument list for the call to fnNode.
   * @param namesNeedingTemps The set of names to update.
   */
Method code:
245 |   static void maybeAddTempsForCallArguments(
246 |       Node fnNode, Map<String, Node> argMap, Set<String> namesNeedingTemps,
247 |       CodingConvention convention) {
248 |     if (argMap.isEmpty()) {
249 |       // No arguments to check, we are done.
250 |       return;
251 |     }
252 | 
253 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(fnNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
254 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |     Set<String> parameters = argMap.keySet();
257 | 
258 |     // Get the list of parameters that may need temporaries due to
259 |     // side-effects.
260 |     Set<String> namesAfterSideEffects = findParametersReferencedAfterSideEffect(
261 |         parameters, block);
262 | 
263 |     // Check for arguments that are evaluated more than once.
264 |     for (Map.Entry<String, Node> entry : argMap.entrySet()) {
265 |       String argName = entry.getKey();
266 |       if (namesNeedingTemps.contains(argName)) {
267 |         continue;
268 |       }
269 |       Node cArg = entry.getValue();
270 |       boolean safe = true;
271 |       int references = NodeUtil.getNameReferenceCount(block, argName);
272 | 
273 |       if (NodeUtil.mayEffectMutableState(cArg) && references > 0) {
274 |         // Note: Mutable arguments should be assigned to temps, as the
275 |         // may be within in a loop:
276 |         //   function x(a) {
277 |         //     for(var i=0; i<0; i++) {
278 |         //       foo(a);
279 |         //     }
280 |         //   x( [] );
281 |         //
282 |         //   The parameter in the call to foo should not become "[]".
283 |         safe = false;
284 |       } else if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(cArg)) {
285 |         // Even if there are no references, we still need to evaluate the
286 |         // expression if it has side-effects.
287 |         safe = false;
288 |       } else if (NodeUtil.canBeSideEffected(cArg)
289 |           && namesAfterSideEffects.contains(argName)) {
290 |         safe = false;
291 |       } else if (references > 1) {
292 |         // Safe is a misnomer, this is a check for "large".
293 |         switch (cArg.getType()) {
294 |           case Token.NAME:
295 |             String name = cArg.getString();
296 |             safe = !(convention.isExported(name));
297 |             break;
298 |           case Token.THIS:
299 |             safe = true;
300 |             break;
301 |           case Token.STRING:
302 |             safe = (cArg.getString().length() < 2);
303 |             break;
304 |           default:
305 |             safe = NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(cArg);
306 |             break;
307 |         }
308 |       }
309 | 
310 |       if (!safe) {
311 |         namesNeedingTemps.add(argName);
312 |       }
313 |     }
314 |   }

===== M0910 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidateFunctions.findFunctionExpressions(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 212-235
Documentation:
/**
     * Find function expressions that are called directly in the form of
     *   (function(a,b,...){...})(a,b,...)
     * or
     *   (function(a,b,...){...}).call(this,a,b, ...)
     */
Method code:
212 |     public void findFunctionExpressions(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
213 |       switch (n.getType()) {
214 |         // Functions expressions in the form of:
215 |         //   (function(){})();
216 |         case Token.CALL:
217 |           Node fnNode = null;
218 |           if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
219 |             fnNode = n.getFirstChild();
220 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(n)) {
221 |             Node fnIdentifingNode = n.getFirstChild().getFirstChild();
222 |             if (fnIdentifingNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
223 |               fnNode = fnIdentifingNode;
224 |             }
225 |           }
226 | 
227 |           // If a interesting function was discovered, add it.
228 |           if (fnNode != null) {
229 |             Function fn = new FunctionExpression(fnNode, callsSeen++);
230 |             maybeAddFunction(fn, t.getModule());
231 |             anonFns.put(fnNode, fn.getName());
232 |           }
233 |           break;
234 |       }
235 |     }

===== M0914 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.rewriteFunctionDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 120-144
Documentation:
/**
   * @param n The node to inspect
   */
Method code:
120 |   private void rewriteFunctionDeclarations(Node n) {
121 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
122 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
123 |         // Rewrite: function f() {} ==> var f = function() {}
124 |         Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
125 | 
126 |         Node var = new Node(Token.VAR).copyInformationFrom(n);
127 |         Node name = Node.newString(Token.NAME, fnNameNode.getString())
128 |             .copyInformationFrom(fnNameNode);
129 | 
130 |         fnNameNode.setString("");
131 |         // Add the VAR, remove the FUNCTION
132 |         n.getParent().replaceChild(n, var);
133 |         var.addChildToFront(name);
134 |         // readd the function as a function expression
135 |         name.addChildToFront(n);
136 |       }
137 |       return;
138 |     }
139 | 
140 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(), next; c != null; c = next) {
141 |       next = c.getNext(); // We may rewrite "c"
142 |       rewriteFunctionDeclarations(c);
143 |     }
144 |   }

===== M0921 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x && !isNegativeZero(x)) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0938 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 839-859
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the Node is a DO or FUNCTION (with or without
   * labels).
   */
Method code:
839 |   private boolean isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node n) {
840 |     if (n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
841 |       Node labeledStatement = n.getLastChild();
842 |       if (labeledStatement.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
843 |         return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(labeledStatement);
844 |       } else {
845 |         // For labels with block children, we need to ensure that a
846 |         // labeled FUNCTION or DO isn't generated when extraneous BLOCKs
847 |         // are skipped.
848 |         if (getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2) == 1) {
849 |           return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(getFirstNonEmptyChild(n));
850 |         } else {
851 |           // Either a empty statement or an block with more than one child,
852 |           // way it isn't a FUNCTION or DO.
853 |           return false;
854 |         }
855 |       }
856 |     } else {
857 |       return (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION || n.getType() == Token.DO);
858 |     }
859 |   }

===== M0939 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inliningLowersCost(JSModule,Node,Collection<? extends Reference>,Set<String>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 730-786
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine if inlining the function is likely to reduce the code size.
   * @param namesToAlias
   */
Method code:
730 |   boolean inliningLowersCost(
731 |       JSModule fnModule, Node fnNode, Collection<? extends Reference> refs,
732 |       Set<String> namesToAlias, boolean isRemovable, boolean referencesThis) {
733 |     int referenceCount = refs.size();
734 |     if (referenceCount == 0) {
735 |       return true;
736 |     }
737 | 
738 |     int referencesUsingBlockInlining = 0;
739 | 
740 |     boolean checkModules = isRemovable && fnModule != null;
741 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
742 | 
743 |     for (Reference ref : refs) {
744 |       if (ref.mode == InliningMode.BLOCK) {
745 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining++;
746 |       }
747 | 
748 |       // Check if any of the references cross the module boundaries.
749 |       if (checkModules && ref.module != null) {
750 |         if (ref.module != fnModule &&
751 |             !moduleGraph.dependsOn(ref.module, fnModule)) {
752 |           // Calculate the cost as if the function were non-removable,
753 |           // if it still lowers the cost inline it.
754 |           isRemovable = false;
755 |           checkModules = false;  // no need to check additional modules.
756 |         }
757 |       }
758 |     }
759 | 
760 |     int referencesUsingDirectInlining = referenceCount -
761 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining;
762 | 
763 |     // Don't bother calculating the cost of function for simple functions where
764 |     // possible.
765 |     // However, when inlining a complex function, even a single reference may be
766 |     // larger than the original function if there are many returns (resulting
767 |     // in additional assignments) or many parameters that need to be aliased
768 |     // so use the cost estimating.
769 |     if (referenceCount == 1 && isRemovable &&
770 |         referencesUsingDirectInlining == 1) {
771 |       return true;
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     int callCost = estimateCallCost(fnNode, referencesThis);
775 |     int overallCallCost = callCost * referenceCount;
776 | 
777 |     int costDeltaDirect = inlineCostDelta(
778 |         fnNode, namesToAlias, InliningMode.DIRECT);
779 |     int costDeltaBlock = inlineCostDelta(
780 |         fnNode, namesToAlias, InliningMode.BLOCK);
781 | 
782 |     return doesLowerCost(fnNode, overallCallCost,
783 |         referencesUsingDirectInlining, costDeltaDirect,
784 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining, costDeltaBlock,
785 |         isRemovable);
786 |   }

===== M0940 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1005-1075
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1005 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
1006 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
1007 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
1008 |                           String backslashEscape,
1009 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
1010 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1011 |     sb.append(quote);
1012 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1013 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1014 |       switch (c) {
1015 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1016 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1017 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1018 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1019 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1020 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1021 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1022 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1023 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1024 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1025 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1026 |             sb.append("\\>");
1027 |           } else {
1028 |             sb.append(c);
1029 |           }
1030 |           break;
1031 |         case '<':
1032 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1033 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1034 | 
1035 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1036 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1037 | 
1038 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1039 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1040 |             sb.append("<\\");
1041 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1042 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1043 |             sb.append("<\\");
1044 |           } else {
1045 |             sb.append(c);
1046 |           }
1047 |           break;
1048 |         default:
1049 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1050 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1051 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1052 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1053 |               sb.append(c);
1054 |             } else {
1055 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1056 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1057 |             }
1058 |           } else {
1059 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1060 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1061 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1062 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1063 |               sb.append(c);
1064 |             } else {
1065 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1066 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1067 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1068 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1069 |             }
1070 |           }
1071 |       }
1072 |     }
1073 |     sb.append(quote);
1074 |     return sb.toString();
1075 |   }

===== M0951 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidatesReferences.checkNameUsage(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 549-588
Documentation:
/**
     * Find functions that can be inlined.
     */
Method code:
549 |     private void checkNameUsage(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
550 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.NAME);
551 | 
552 |       if (isCandidateUsage(n)) {
553 |         return;
554 |       }
555 | 
556 |       // Other refs to a function name remove its candidacy for inlining
557 |       String name = n.getString();
558 |       FunctionState fs = fns.get(name);
559 |       if (fs == null) {
560 |         return;
561 |       }
562 | 
563 |       // Unlike normal call/new parameters, references passed to
564 |       // JSCompiler_ObjectPropertyString are not aliases of a value, but
565 |       // a reference to the name itself, as such the value of the name is
566 |       // unknown and can not be inlined.
567 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.NEW) {
568 |         Node target = parent.getFirstChild();
569 |         if (target.getType() == Token.NAME && target.getString().equals(
570 |             ObjectPropertyStringPreprocess.EXTERN_OBJECT_PROPERTY_STRING)) {
571 |           // This method is going to be replaced so don't inline it anywhere.
572 |           fs.setInline(false);
573 |         }
574 |       }
575 | 
576 |       // If the name is being assigned to it can not be inlined.
577 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
578 |         // e.g. bar = something; <== we can't inline "bar"
579 |         // so mark the function as uninlinable.
580 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Should we just remove it from fns here?
581 |         fs.setInline(false);
582 |       } else {
583 |         // e.g. var fn = bar; <== we can't inline "bar"
584 |         // As this reference can't be inlined mark the function as
585 |         // unremovable.
586 |         fs.setRemove(false);
587 |       }
588 |     }

===== M0959 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1938-1952
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1938 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1939 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1940 |       return getString();
1941 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1942 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1943 |       if (left == null) {
1944 |         return null;
1945 |       }
1946 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1947 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1948 |       return "this";
1949 |     } else {
1950 |       return null;
1951 |     }
1952 |   }

2773 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2774 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2775 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2776 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2777 |       return true;
2778 |     }

2781 |       return false;
2782 |     }

2788 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
117 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
118 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
119 | 
120 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
121 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
123 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
127 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
128 |     }
129 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

===== M0970 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

===== M0971 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.ContextualRenameInverter.exitScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 307-328
Documentation:
/**
     * Rename vars for the current scope, and merge any referenced
     * names into the parent scope reference set.
     */
Method code:
307 |     @Override
308 |     public void exitScope(NodeTraversal t) {
309 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
310 |         return;
311 |       }
312 | 
313 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
314 |         Var v = it.next();
315 |         handleScopeVar(v);
316 |       }
317 | 
318 |       // Merge any names that were referenced but not declared in the current
319 |       // scope.
320 |       Set<String> current = referencedNames;
321 |       referencedNames = referenceStack.pop();
322 |       // If there isn't anything left in the stack we will be going into the
323 |       // global scope: don't try to build a set of referenced names for the
324 |       // global scope.
325 |       if (!referenceStack.isEmpty()) {
326 |         referencedNames.addAll(current);
327 |       }
328 |     }

638 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
639 |       }
640 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
641 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
642 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
643 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
644 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
645 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
646 |       for (JSType alternate : type.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
647 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
648 |       }
649 |     }
650 |   }

===== M0985 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.isSubexpressionMovable(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 730-821
Documentation:
/**
   * Walk the AST from the call site to the expression root and verify that
   * the portions of the expression that are evaluated before the call are:
   * 1) Unaffected by the the side-efects, if any, of the call.
   * 2) That there are no side-effects, that may influence the call.
   *
   * For example, if x has side-effects:
   *   a = 1 + x();
   * the call to x can be moved because "a" final value of a can not be
   * influenced by x(), but in:
   *   a = b + x();
   * the call to x can not be moved because the value of b may be modified
   * by the call to x.
   *
   * If x is without side-effects in:
   *   a = b + x();
   * the call to x can be moved, but in:
   *   a = (b.foo = c) + x();
   * the call to x can not be moved because the value of b.foo may be referenced
   * by x().  Note: this is true even if b is a local variable; the object that
   * b refers to may have a global alias.
   *
   * @return UNDECOMPOSABLE if the expression can not be moved, DECOMPOSABLE if
   * decomposition is required before the expression can be moved, otherwise
   * MOVABLE.
   */
Method code:
730 |   private DecompositionType isSubexpressionMovable(
731 |       Node expressionRoot, Node subExpression) {
732 |     boolean requiresDecomposition = false;
733 |     boolean seenSideEffects = NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(subExpression);
734 | 
735 |     Node child = subExpression;
736 |     for (Node parent : child.getAncestors()) {
737 |       if (parent == expressionRoot) {
738 |         // Done. The walk back to the root of the expression is complete, and
739 |         // nothing was encountered that blocks the call from being moved.
740 |         return requiresDecomposition
741 |             ? DecompositionType.DECOMPOSABLE
742 |             : DecompositionType.MOVABLE;
743 |       }
744 | 
745 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
746 | 
747 |       if (isConditionalOp(parent)) {
748 |         // Only the first child is always executed, otherwise it must be
749 |         // decomposed.
750 |         if (child != parent.getFirstChild()) {
751 |           requiresDecomposition = true;
752 |         }
753 |       } else {
754 |         // Only inline the call if none of the preceding siblings in the
755 |         // expression have side-effects, and are unaffected by the side-effects,
756 |         // if any, of the call in question.
757 |         // NOTE: This depends on the siblings being in the same order as they
758 |         // are evaluated.
759 | 
760 |         // SPECIAL CASE: Assignment to a simple name
761 |         if (isSafeAssign(parent, seenSideEffects)) {
762 |           // It is always safe to inline "foo()" for expressions such as
763 |           //   "a = b = c = foo();"
764 |           // As the assignment is unaffected by side effect of "foo()"
765 |           // and the names assigned-to can not influence the state before
766 |           // the call to foo.
767 |           //
768 |           // This is not true of more complex LHS values, such as
769 |           //    a.x = foo();
770 |           //    next().x = foo();
771 |           // in these cases the checks below are necessary.
772 |         } else {
773 |           // Everything else.
774 |           for (Node n : parent.children()) {
775 |             if (n == child) {
776 |               // None of the preceding siblings have side-effects.
777 |               // This is OK.
778 |               break;
779 |             }
780 | 
781 |             if (isExpressionTreeUnsafe(
782 |                 n, seenSideEffects)) {
783 |               seenSideEffects = true;
784 |               requiresDecomposition = true;
785 |             }
786 |           }
787 | 
788 |           // In Internet Explorer, DOM objects and other external objects
789 |           // methods can not be called indirectly, as is required when the
790 |           // object or its property can be side-effected.  For example,
791 |           // when exposing expression f() (with side-effects) in: x.m(f())
792 |           // either the value of x or its property m might have changed, so
793 |           // both the 'this' value ('x') and the function to be called ('x.m')
794 |           // need to be preserved. Like so:
795 |           //   var t1 = x, t2 = x.m, t3 = f();
796 |           //   t2.call(t1, t3);
797 |           // As IE doesn't support the call to these non-javascript objects
798 |           // methods in this way. We can't do this.
799 |           // We don't currently distinguish between these types of objects
800 |           // in the extern definitions and if we did we would need accurate
801 |           // type information.
802 |           //
803 |           Node first = parent.getFirstChild();
804 |           if (requiresDecomposition
805 |               && parent.getType() == Token.CALL
806 |               && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
807 |             if (maybeExternMethod(first)) {
808 |               return DecompositionType.UNDECOMPOSABLE;
809 |             } else {
810 |               return DecompositionType.DECOMPOSABLE;
811 |             }
812 |           }
813 |         }
814 |       }
815 |       // Continue looking up the expression tree.
816 |       child = parent;
817 |     }
818 | 
819 |     // With a valid tree we should never get here.
820 |     throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected.");
821 |   }

===== M0994 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.isSafeAssign(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 844-858
Documentation:
/**
   * It is always safe to inline "foo()" for expressions such as
   *    "a = b = c = foo();"
   * As the assignment is unaffected by side effect of "foo()"
   * and the names assigned-to can not influence the state before
   * the call to foo.
   *
   * It is also safe in cases like where the object is constant:
   *    CONST_NAME.a = foo()
   *    CONST_NAME[CONST_VALUE] = foo();
   *
   * This is not true of more complex LHS values, such as
   *     a.x = foo();
   *     next().x = foo();
   * in these cases the checks below are necessary.
   *
   * @param seenSideEffects If true, check to see if node-tree maybe affected by
   * side-effects, otherwise if the tree has side-effects. @see
   * isExpressionTreeUnsafe
   * @return Whether the assignment is safe from side-effects.
   */
Method code:
844 |   private boolean isSafeAssign(Node n, boolean seenSideEffects) {
845 |     if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
846 |       Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
847 |       switch (lhs.getType()) {
848 |         case Token.NAME:
849 |           return true;
850 |         case Token.GETPROP:
851 |           return !isExpressionTreeUnsafe(lhs.getFirstChild(), seenSideEffects);
852 |         case Token.GETELEM:
853 |           return !isExpressionTreeUnsafe(lhs.getFirstChild(), seenSideEffects)
854 |               && !isExpressionTreeUnsafe(lhs.getLastChild(), seenSideEffects);
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |     return false;
858 |   }

     * shouldTraverse is call when descending into the Node tree, so it is used
     * here to build the context for label renames.
     *
     * {@inheritDoc}

Method code:
136 |     @Override
137 |     public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node node,
138 |         Node parent) {
139 |       if (node.getType() == Token.LABEL) {

===== M1007 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.mimimizeCost(FunctionState)

Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the function is worth inlining and potentially
   * trims references that increase the cost.
   * @return Whether inlining the references lowers the overall cost.
   */
Method code:
689 |   private boolean mimimizeCost(FunctionState fs) {
690 |     if (!inliningLowersCost(fs)) {
691 |       // Try again without Block inlining references
692 |       if (fs.hasBlockInliningReferences()) {
693 |         fs.setRemove(false);
